Values of Free schemas (CostSectionType 900) are omitted with a connector warning, never returned as a search key (D8).DerivedGuide: Data model: 'a cost section is a value within a schema (e.g. OFFICE )'; POST /cost-schemas.list → '900: Free - expected values are free'; POST /cost-sections.list → Filter.IncludeInactive: '*FALSE* by default'
items[].lines[].analytic_distribution[].analytic_accounts[].percentage req.CostDetail.CostLines[].Debit / Credit vs line Debit / CreditTransformHorus stores amounts, so convert them to whole-number percentages (Chift: 'Only whole numbers... must equal 100%'). Use largest-remainder rounding, the cross-route convention of D8; a 33.33 / 33.33 / 33.34 split becomes 33 / 33 / 34, so precision is lost.DerivedPOST /account-histories.list → CostLines[]: 'Debit: number|null', 'Credit: number|null [example=1445.09]'; POST /invoices.purchases.new → CostLines[].Amount: 'The sum of all cost lines' amounts must be equal to the corresponding invoice line's base amount.'
Detailed open questions (21)
  • Horus For GET /book-entries.info: what does it return for an Id that was replaced by an update or deleted (404 or the old entry)? Does the guide's 'full detail' mean anything beyond the documented header (for example lines or VAT inline)? Can sideloading (CompanyDetail.Company, AccountHistories) be enabled on it, as Guide: Sideloading invites ('If you need it on another endpoint, contact us')? Single-invoice reads and the attachments route use book-entries.info by Id. Its documented response equals a book-entries.list item with line references only, so a single read still needs account-histories.list (with a month window) and companies.info, and the 404 behaviour decides how stale Ids are reported.
  • Horus Can a credit note (Type 1) be posted into a 200/100 daybook, and an invoice into 250/150? Is the document Type (invoice / credit note) exposed anywhere on read besides invoices.sales.outstanding.list TransactionType? D1 makes the sign-based classification over both sibling daybook types mandatory and labels it A; a Horus answer, or a read-side Type, would confirm or replace the rule and could halve the book-entries.list calls.
  • Horus Do StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear filter on AccountingMonth/AccountingYear or on Date? Is there a maximum span, a response size limit or a rate limit? Day-level date filtering, window widening and caching strategy depend on it; no rate limits are documented.
  • Horus On book-entries.list, does Filter.ModifiedAfter apply to the entry or to its account histories (the description says 'account-histories')? Does matching or unmatching update the LastModifiedDate of the entry and/or of the partner account-history line? If matching updates the line, account-histories.list ModifiedAfter on partner lines could catch newly paid invoices without a full open-items refresh.
  • Horus Which relationships can be sideloaded with Include on book-entries.list and account-histories.list (AccountHistories? Document? CompanyDetail.Company on lines? nested Ibans)? Do Included entities carry the full companies.list schema? Sideloading AccountHistories could merge the header and line calls, Ibans would remove the N+1 for bank_accounts, and partial entities would force a companies.list fallback.
  • Horus What exactly are CompanyDetail.TotalAmount, TurnoverAmount, TaxAmount, RemarkPayment, RemarkCompany and PaymentDate (signs for credit notes, currency, mapping to the write fields)? These are the header sources, but their descriptions are empty, so the mappings are assumptions. PaymentDate could also be a paid indicator.
  • Horus Does invoices.sales.outstanding.list ever return fully paid invoices (PaymentStatus 800 'Paid')? Is a purchase-side equivalent planned? This affects whether payments and paid status can come from one endpoint or must be rebuilt from matching.
  • Horus Can Horus provide the accepted tax-combination files (sales and purchases), and are they versioned? Is VatDetail filled on base lines, VAT lines or both? The D2 synthetic VAT catalogue is built from these files and lines[].tax_code is returned only when exactly one catalogue entry matches; tax_rate and tax_amount allocation depend on where VatDetail sits.
  • Horus Direction and meaning of CurrencyRate; currency of CompanyDetail amounts (and of account-history Debit/Credit). Is the folder base currency always EUR? D4 assumes folder-currency EUR everywhere and returns EUR with currency 'EUR' when invoice-currency amounts are not available; confirmation would let foreign-currency invoices be returned in their own currency.
  • Horus Is there any way to detect deleted or replaced book entries (tombstones, webhooks)? Guide: Changes & versioning mentions webhook types, but no webhook documentation was provided. Without it, consumers cannot remove stale invoices on an incremental sync.
  • Horus What do CustomerActivitySuspended (companies) and IsUsed (daybooks) mean exactly? The shared partner mapper (D9) uses active = NOT CustomerActivitySuspended (assumption), and IsUsed decides whether unused daybooks can safely be skipped.
  • Chift Should unconfirmed Horus entries (Confirmed=false, accountant review pending) be returned as status 'draft', or excluded by default? API-pushed entries are unconfirmed by default, so this changes what consumers see right after they create an invoice.
  • Chift D1 returns an entry found in a sales daybook as customer_refund when its customer line is a net credit: is that acceptable for consumers who filter on journal_id? Should refund amounts be returned positive? Is 'paid' acceptable for an invoice fully offset by a credit note? It affects the invoice_type filter semantics, the sign conventions and the payment_status meaning that consumers rely on.
  • Chift Should lines be returned even when include_invoice_lines=false, given that Horus needs no extra request for them? The contract allows omission only 'when this requires extra requests'; the choice trades consistency against payload size.
  • Chift Is it acceptable that invoice id changes when the accountant updates the invoice, or should the connector expose a composite business key? Id stability affects idempotency and the follow-up GET /accounting/attachments call, which resolves the Document through GET /book-entries.info?Id={invoice_id} and may fail once that Id has been replaced.
  • Chift What default period applies when date_from/date_to are absent, given that Horus requires a month window and has no pagination? How far back should the payment search start (advance payments)? It drives cost and latency; a full-history scan per page request is unrealistic.
  • Chift How should approval_status (filter and field) behave for a target without an approval workflow: always 'unknown' and reject the filter, or map Verified to 'approved'? Horus has only an optional Verified flag.
  • Horus Where does the invoice header Comment surface on read (partner-line Comment, RemarkCompany, Remark)? D3 reads reference and customer_memo by splitting the header Comment; the read order (partner-line Comment, then RemarkCompany, then the sales Remark) is an assumption until Horus answers.
  • Horus Can TaxKind be exposed on read (in account-histories VatDetail)? What does the read-only TaxCondition value '4 = Article' correspond to on write? Without TaxKind several purchase VAT codes can match the same VatDetail, so D2 returns tax_code = null; TaxCondition 4 has no write-side equivalent and never matches the catalogue.
  • Chift Is omitting Free-schema analytics (CostSectionType 900) from invoice lines acceptable in v1, or should a later version expose them (for example with a documented 'schemaId|value' id)? D8 excludes Free schemas from Chift analytics, so their cost lines are dropped from analytic_distribution with only a connector warning.
  • Chift For foreign-currency sales invoices, should the connector use the invoice-currency amounts while the item is open (they disappear from Horus once it is closed), or always return a stable EUR representation? Under D4 the same invoice can switch from its own currency to EUR between two syncs, which may confuse consumers that compare amounts.
Invoice creation (multiple analytic plans)Supported with workaroundPOST /consumers/{consumer_id}/accounting/invoices/multi-analytic-plansNative creation with invoices.sales.new or invoices.purchases.new, PDF included. VAT codes, analytics and numbering need connector logic; the entry is read back after creation.

Customer invoices and credit notes go to invoices.sales.new, supplier ones to invoices.purchases.new, with the PDF in the same call. Before posting, the connector resolves the daybook, partner, accounts and cost sections from cached reference data, translates tax_code into Horus VAT fields, converts analytic percentages into amounts and checks the document number. After posting it reads the entry back. Main risks: the VAT combination catalogue, numeric numbering without automatic numbers, and the confirmation default.

Detailed analysis

Horus has two native write endpoints for this route. POST /invoices.sales.new takes customer_invoice (Type=0) and customer_refund (Type=1). POST /invoices.purchases.new takes supplier_invoice (Type=0) and supplier_refund (Type=1). Both accept header totals, VAT-qualified lines, multi-schema cost lines, a currency and the PDF in one multipart call. Horus's own posting guide lists the four prerequisites the connector must resolve first: FolderId, a 200/250 or 100/150 DaybookId, a found-or-created CompanyId, and the account search keys.

One Chift call therefore becomes several Horus calls:

  • Folder (D7): folder_id is required when the connection has more than one selected folder; otherwise the single selected folder, or the single folder from folders.list, is used.
  • Cached lookups per folder: daybooks (D1 filter: customer_invoice → 200; customer_refund → 250, else 200; supplier_invoice → 100; supplier_refund → 150, else 100; exactly one candidate or 400 'journal_id required'), accounts, the analytics cache (cost schemas + cost sections including inactive ones, D8), fiscal years (closure dates and IsClosed) and the folder.
  • Partner: partner_id is folder-checked with companies.info (D13). partner_info is treated as the company SearchKey (assumption, D10): companies.list, then companies.update to set a missing IsCustomer/IsSupplier flag, or companies.new if no match.
  • Duplicate pre-check (D12): book-entries.list on DocumentNo whenever invoice_number is supplied, and again before any retry. Horus documents no uniqueness or idempotency key and offers no invoice delete.
  • The create call itself.
  • Read-back (skipped when ignore_accounting_id=true): create returns only {Id}, so GET /book-entries.info?Id=… reads the entry back in one call with no period and its Folder.Id is checked (D13). POST /account-histories.list filtered on BookEntryId is added only when line-level data is needed (the EUR partner-line amount of a foreign-currency invoice per D4, the partner collective account).

The biggest risk is VAT. Chift sends tax_code as 'the Id of the Tax Code', but Horus has no VAT-code entity. Each line needs TaxRate + TaxOrigin + TaxType (purchases add TaxKind/TaxCondition/TaxConditionValue), and the valid combinations are in reference files that were not provided. The connector therefore owns a synthetic VAT-code catalogue with one canonical id grammar shared with GET vat-codes and Invoice retrieval (D2): 'S-{TaxRate}-{TaxOrigin}-{TaxType}' (+ '-{TaxCountryCode}' for OSS) and 'P-{TaxRate}-{TaxOrigin}-{TaxType}-{TaxKind}-{TaxCondition}-{TaxConditionValue}'.

Other risks:

  • Per-plan percentages must become joint cost-line amounts (maximum 8 schemas), rounded with the largest-remainder method; Free schemas are excluded in v1 (D8).
  • invoice_number must be numeric.
  • API entries are unconfirmed by default so the accountant can review them. posted maps to Confirmed=true unless the per-consumer 'always push unconfirmed' setting is on (D12).
  • Credit notes may fall back to a 200/100 daybook (D1), which Horus has not confirmed it accepts.
  • reference and customer_memo share the header Comment ('{reference} | {customer_memo}', D3), so reference is lost as a structured field.
  • Several Chift fields have no Horus field: withholding_tax, nl_payment_terms_split, start/end/delivery dates, force_financial_period, payment_term_id, and line quantity/unit price.

Operationally, every retry must first run the duplicate pre-check, token refreshes must be serialised (refresh tokens are single-use), and all write tests must use a test folder, because the API works on the license's real data.

Horus endpoints

  • PrimaryPOST /invoices.sales.new Creates the sale invoice (customer_invoice: Type=0) or sale credit note (customer_refund: Type=1). Horus converts it into a book entry and returns only its Id. Use application/json without a PDF, or multipart/form-data (file + body) with a PDF. (1 per Chift call (customer_* types))
  • PrimaryPOST /invoices.purchases.new Creates the purchase invoice (supplier_invoice: Type=0) or purchase credit note (supplier_refund: Type=1), with the same JSON and multipart variants. Purchase lines add TaxKind/TaxCondition/TaxConditionValue, VehicleId and PersonalShare. Number is nullable here. (1 per Chift call (supplier_* types))
  • LookupPOST /folders.list Resolves FolderId under the shared folder rule (D7): folder_id is required whenever the connection has more than one selected folder; if exactly one folder was selected at activation, that folder is used without this call; if no selection is stored, use the single folder returned by POST /folders.list {Mode: 0}; otherwise 400 'folder_id required'. A license holds several folders. Also returns WithAnalytics and Legislation (BE/LU). (cached reference data (only when folder_id is missing and no activation selection is stored))
  • LookupGET /folders.info Supplies Language and CountryCode as defaults when creating a partner from partner_info (Language is the D9 write-side LanguageCode fallback and may be 'XX'), and WithAnalytics to accept or reject analytic_distribution. (cached reference data, 1 per folder)
  • LookupPOST /daybooks.list Resolves DaybookId when journal_id is null, with the per-invoice_type filter of D1: customer_invoice → Filter.Types [200]; customer_refund → [250], falling back to [200] if the folder has no 250 daybook; supplier_invoice → [100]; supplier_refund → [150], falling back to [100]. Exactly one candidate daybook must remain, otherwise 400 'journal_id required'. When journal_id is given, the cached, folder-scoped list validates that the daybook exists in the folder (which also enforces D13) and that its Type belongs to the invoice side (200/250 for customer_*, 100/150 for supplier_*). Also gives the daybook SearchKey (e.g. VEN), which the BookEntryNumber format uses. (cached reference data, 1 per folder)
  • AlternativeGET /daybooks.info Alternative to the cached daybooks.list for checking one given journal_id: Data.Type must belong to the invoice side and Data.Folder.Id must equal folder_id, otherwise 400 (D13). (0-1 per Chift call (or cached))
  • LookupGET /companies.info partner_id guard (recommended, cached per partner_id): Data.Folder.Id must equal folder_id, otherwise 400 (D13); IsCustomer (sales) or IsSupplier (purchases) is checked (see the missing-flag edge case). Optional source for accounting_info.account_number (CustomerConcentrationAccount/SupplierConcentrationAccount). Source of response.partner when enrichment is enabled, mapped with the shared D9 mapper. Optional, off by default: UsualCustomerPaymentMode/UsualSupplierPaymentMode as a PaymentMode fallback. (0-1 per Chift call (cached per partner_id; recommended whenever partner_id is given))
  • OptionalPOST /companies.list partner_info flow (partner_id null, D10): finds an existing company by SearchKey = partner_info.account_number, sanitised with Client creation's SearchKey rules. The request is FolderId-scoped; the filter uses contains, so post-filter on an exact SearchKey match (unique within the folder) and reuse a match only if Data[].Folder.Id = folder_id. The match's IsCustomer/IsSupplier flags decide whether companies.update is needed. Companies created by the connector can also be found again reliably with Filter.ExternalReference. (0-1 per Chift call (only when partner_info is used))
  • OptionalPOST /companies.new partner_info flow (D10): creates the missing customer or supplier with Client creation's required-field rules: SearchKey = sanitised account_number, Name = account_name, IsCustomer/IsSupplier from invoice_type, IsVatLiable=false, LanguageCode = folders.info Language (D9 fallback, may be 'XX'), CountryCode = folders.info CountryCode. Returns the CompanyId the invoice uses. (0-1 per Chift call (only when partner_info is used and no match exists))
  • OptionalPOST /companies.update partner_info flow (D10): when the company found by SearchKey lacks the role flag for the invoice side, set only that flag (IsCustomer=true for customer_*, IsSupplier=true for supplier_*). force_merge logic: send CompanyId plus the flag, leave every other field null or omitted, never "" (an empty string erases the stored value). (0-1 per Chift call (only when the matched company lacks the role flag))
  • LookupPOST /accounts.list Validates that each lines[].account_number and invoice_correction account exists and is active: exact SearchKey match after the starts-with filter, Inactive=false. Optionally rejects IsSummary accounts (assumption) and cross-checks the line rate against VatRate (derived). (cached reference data, 1 per folder)
  • LookupPOST /cost-schemas.list First half of the per-folder analytics cache (D8). Maps the Chift analytic_plan (= cost schema GUID Id, D8) to the Horus SchemaSearchKey sent on posting. Also returns Mandatory, CostSectionType (100 List / 900 Free, with FreeCostSectionType/Mask) and OrderNo. Free schemas (900) are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 and rejected on write (D8). No per-call POST /cost-schemas.info. (cached reference data, 1 per folder)
  • LookupPOST /cost-sections.list Second half of the D8 cache: one call per List-type schema (CostSectionType 100) with Filter.IncludeInactive = true, so inactive sections are known and rejected with 422 instead of surfacing as 'not found'. Maps the Chift analytic_account (= cost section GUID Id, D8) to the section SearchKey sent as Costs[].Value, and checks that the section belongs to the plan's schema. IsLocked is exposed, but its posting behaviour is undocumented. No per-call POST /cost-sections.info (D8). (cached reference data, 1 per List-type cost schema (max 8 schemas per folder))
  • LookupPOST /fiscal-years.list Pre-validation recommended by Horus: invoice_date falls in an existing fiscal year (Filter.Dates, IncludeFuture) and after LastSalesClosureDate/LastPurchasesClosureDate/LastVatClosureDate. Also gives the period bounds for the book-entries.list and account-histories.list calls. (cached reference data, 1 per folder)
  • LookupGET /fiscal-years.info Checks IsClosed for the fiscal year containing invoice_date (Id from fiscal-years.list). It is the only endpoint that exposes IsClosed. (cached, 1 per fiscal year)
  • LookupPOST /book-entries.list (a) Duplicate pre-check (D12), run whenever invoice_number is supplied: FolderId, DaybookId, the fiscal-year period and Filter.DocumentNo = Number, before creating. Unconfirmed entries are included by default. If one exists, return 409. The same check runs before any retry after a timeout or 500 (never blind-retry). (b) Emulated next number (max DocumentNo + 1) when a posted sale has no invoice_number. (c) No longer needed for the read-back: GET /book-entries.info returns the same entry fields for one entry by Id, without DaybookId or period. book-entries.list stays the only way to find an entry by DocumentNo, because the Id is unknown before creation and after a timeout. (1 per Chift call when invoice_number is supplied (duplicate pre-check / numbering), +1 before any retry)
  • OptionalPOST /account-histories.list Conditional line-level read-back, added to GET /book-entries.info only when a response field needs line data: the EUR amount of a foreign-currency invoice (partner line Debit/Credit, assumed to be in EUR under D4) and the CurrencyRate actually applied, the partner collective account (AccountSearchKey of the AccountType 100/200 line), or booked VatDetail/CostDetail if the connector returns booked values instead of echoes. Filter.BookEntryId over the fiscal-year bounds, with IncludeVatDetail/IncludeCostDetail/IncludeCompanyDetail. One call returns all lines of the entry, which is cheaper than one account-histories.info call per line. Optionally sideload the partner with Include "CompanyDetail.Company". VatDetail is returned only for VAT-liable folders and CostDetail only for folders with analytics. (0-1 per Chift call (only when currency≠EUR, accounting_info.account_number is filled, or booked line detail is wanted; skipped when ignore_accounting_id=true))
  • EnrichmentGET /book-entries.info Default read-back after creation: one GET by the Id that create returns, with no FolderId, DaybookId or period parameter. Data.Folder.Id must equal folder_id, otherwise 400 (D13 cross-folder guard). It returns the same entry-level fields as a book-entries.list item: DocumentNo, Confirmed, Verified, Date, LastModifiedDate, CompanyDetail (Company, RemarkPayment, RemarkCompany, DueDate, TotalAmount, TurnoverAmount, TaxAmount), Folder, Daybook, Document, and AccountHistories as references only (Type + Id). The CompanyDetail amounts are assumed to be in EUR (D4, A), so they are never paired with a foreign currency code. It has no line amounts, VAT or cost detail, currency, CurrencyRate or account search keys; those need account-histories.list. It is not on the guide's list of sideloading endpoints (derived). It cannot replace the duplicate pre-check, because the Id is unknown before creation and after a timeout. (0-1 per Chift call (default read-back; skipped when ignore_accounting_id=true))
  • AlternativeGET /account-histories.info Period-independent fallback for line data: reads one account history line by Id (the Ids come from GET /book-entries.info → AccountHistories[]). Returns AccountSearchKey, AccountType, Debit/Credit, CurrencyCode/CurrencyRate, VatDetail and CostDetail (the latter only for folders with costs) without Include flags or a period. It costs one call per line, so use it only when account-histories.list with Filter.BookEntryId returns nothing (e.g. the entry was booked outside the queried period). (0-N per Chift call (1 per booked line; fallback only))
  • AlternativePOST /book-entries.documents.new Fallback way to attach the PDF in a second call (JSON create, then attach). It fails if a document is already attached. The single multipart create call is preferred because the invoice and its PDF are created together. (0-1 per Chift call)
  • OptionalGET /documents.info Optional: OriginalFileName of the attached document, to fill attachments_info once GET /book-entries.info gives the Document Id. (0-1 per Chift call)
  • AlternativeGET /invoices.sales.outstanding.info Sales-only alternative, superseded by GET /book-entries.info for the header read-back (Confirmed, DueDate, TotalAmount, payment remark), which covers both sales and purchases. Remaining use: TotalAmount next to TotalCurrencyAmount for a foreign-currency sale, as a by-Id alternative source for main_currency_total (D4 keeps the EUR partner-line amount as the primary source). Lookup by BookEntryId, or by BookEntryNumber in the format {DaybookSearchKey}-{Number}. The 'Id' query parameter is almost certainly the FolderId (derived: the guide requires FolderId, and Id is the only remaining parameter). It covers OPEN sale invoices/credit notes only and has no purchase equivalent. (0-1 per Chift call (sales only))
  • OptionalPOST /vehicles.list Not used by default; Chift has no vehicle field. Optional per-consumer default VehicleId for purchases booked on accounts that require a vehicle. (cached reference data (only if a default vehicle is configured))
  • Follow-upPOST /invoices.sales.update and /invoices.purchases.update Not called by this route. Listed because any later correction or draft-to-posted change goes through them: full replacement, a NEW book entry Id, and the original attachment deleted. (not used by this route)
  • AlternativePOST /documents.new Not recommended. It is an automatic-processing pipeline: the document is turned into a book entry by Horus, which would discard Chift's structured data and return a document Id instead of a book entry Id. (not used)

Call sequence

  1. Connection context (Chift-internal). consumer_id → the stored Horus connection: one per license, holding api_url, access token, refresh token and expiry. Access tokens last 1 hour. Refresh proactively a few minutes before expiry, under a per-connection lock, and persist the new token pair before using it, because refresh tokens are single-use (Guide: Token lifecycle). Send Content-Encoding: gzip (Guide: Making requests) and ignore unknown response properties (Guide: Changes & versioning). FolderId (D7): folder_id when given; if it is null and exactly one folder was selected at activation, that folder; if no selection is stored, the single folder returned by POST /folders.list {Mode: 0}; otherwise 400 'folder_id required'.
  2. Chift-side validation, before any Horus call: - total = untaxed_amount + tax_amount on the header and on each line. - Header totals = sum of the lines; otherwise invoice_correction is needed. - Analytic percentages are integers summing to 100 per plan, with at most 8 plans. - Every tax_code (and correction tax code) is a D2 catalogue id with the prefix of the invoice side (S- for customer_*, P- for supplier_*). - The PDF type is one of Pdf/Xml/CODA/SODA/jpg/jpeg/png. - Reject unsupported inputs with 400/422: withholding_tax, nl_payment_terms_split, a force_financial_period that differs from the invoice_date month, a currency outside the Horus enum, a non-numeric invoice_number.
  3. If regroup_lines='true' (the default), merge lines that share (account_number, tax_code, analytic_distribution), summing untaxed_amount, tax_amount and total.
  4. Reference data, loaded once and cached per folder (Guide: Reading accounting data): - POST /daybooks.list: resolve DaybookId with the D1 filter (customer_invoice [200]; customer_refund [250], else [200]; supplier_invoice [100]; supplier_refund [150], else [100]); exactly one candidate, otherwise 400 'journal_id required'. A given journal_id must be in this folder-scoped list with a Type of the invoice side. - POST /accounts.list: exact search key, Inactive=false, optionally IsSummary=false. - Analytics cache (D8): POST /cost-schemas.list, then POST /cost-sections.list per List-type schema with Filter.IncludeInactive = true. Map GUID ids to search keys; read Mandatory and CostSectionType. - POST /fiscal-years.list, then GET /fiscal-years.info: fiscal year bounds, closure dates and IsClosed for invoice_date. Horus recommends this closure check (Guide: Data model). - GET /folders.info: WithAnalytics, Language, CountryCode.
  5. Partner (Guide: Managing customers & suppliers: 'find or create the company first'). - partner_id set: CompanyId = partner_id. Guard with GET /companies.info (cached): Data.Folder.Id must equal folder_id, otherwise 400 (D13); check IsCustomer/IsSupplier for the invoice side. - partner_id null and partner_info set (D10): SearchKey = account_number sanitised with Client creation's rules. POST /companies.list (FolderId, Filter.SearchKey), then post-filter on an exact match in the same folder. If the match lacks IsCustomer (customer_*) or IsSupplier (supplier_*), POST /companies.update with CompanyId + that flag only (never "" values). If no match, POST /companies.new with Client creation's required-field rules (LanguageCode = folder Language per D9, CountryCode = folder CountryCode, IsVatLiable=false, optional ExternalReference), and use the returned Id as CompanyId. - Both null: reject, because CompanyId is required.
  6. VAT (D2): decode each lines[].tax_code, and the correction tax code, through the connector's catalogue: - Sales 'S-{TaxRate}-{TaxOrigin}-{TaxType}' → TaxRate, TaxOrigin, TaxType; OSS ids (TaxOrigin=13) carry a fifth component → TaxCountryCode. - Purchases 'P-{TaxRate}-{TaxOrigin}-{TaxType}-{TaxKind}-{TaxCondition}-{TaxConditionValue}' → the six fields, 'x' → null. An id not in the catalogue → 422. Check the decoded rate against lines[].tax_rate.
  7. Numbering and duplicate pre-check (D12). Number = int(invoice_number). - invoice_number set: POST /book-entries.list (FolderId, DaybookId, fiscal-year months, Filter.DocumentNo=Number) before creating. Unconfirmed entries are included by default. If an entry exists, return 409 Conflict (or the existing entry, per Chift policy). This follows Horus's advice to reconcile on daybook + entry number. - invoice_number null: a draft gets Number=0. A posted sale gets max(DocumentNo)+1 from the same call (assumption, race risk) or is rejected. A posted purchase gets null (nullable).
  8. Build the Horus body. - Header: Type, Number, Date, DueDate, CompanyId, totals, PaymentReferences, PaymentMode, CurrencyCode/CurrencyRate, OverrideTaxAmounts=true, AllowUnbalanced=false. - Comment (D3): '{reference}', '{reference} | {customer_memo}' or '| {customer_memo}'. - Confirmed (D12): false for draft; true for posted unless the per-consumer 'always push unconfirmed' setting is on. - InvoiceLines with CostLines: percentages become amounts as a product of combinations across plans, rounded with the largest-remainder method so the cost lines sum exactly to BaseAmount (D8). - Append an invoice_correction line when header and lines differ.
  9. Create: POST /invoices.sales.new (customer_*) or POST /invoices.purchases.new (supplier_*). Without a PDF, use application/json. With a PDF, use multipart/form-data: file = the base64-decoded bytes, body = the JSON as a plain text form field, including FileName with its extension. Error handling (Guide: Responses & errors): - 400: never retry as-is. Map to Chift 400/422 and surface ErrorType and Details. - 404: map to Chift 404. - 401: refresh once under the lock and retry. If the refresh fails, mark the connection disconnected. - 403: map to Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' (D7). - 500 or timeout: before any retry, re-run the step-6 DocumentNo pre-check (D12), because Horus has no idempotency key and no invoice delete. Then retry with exponential backoff. Never auto-retry a Number=0 draft.
  10. If ignore_accounting_id=true: return the echoed request plus id = the Horus Id right away. Horus is synchronous, so the Id is already known.
  11. Otherwise, read back with GET /book-entries.info?Id=<Id>: one call by Id, with no FolderId, DaybookId or period. Check Data.Folder.Id = folder_id (D13). It gives DocumentNo, Confirmed, Date, LastModifiedDate (last_updated_on), CompanyDetail (Company, DueDate, TotalAmount, TurnoverAmount, TaxAmount, RemarkPayment), Daybook and Document. Its CompanyDetail amounts are assumed EUR (D4) and are never returned under a foreign currency code. Add POST /account-histories.list (FolderId, the fiscal-year period, Filter.BookEntryId=Id, IncludeVatDetail, IncludeCostDetail, IncludeCompanyDetail, optionally Include='CompanyDetail.Company') only when a field needs line data: the EUR partner-line amount and applied CurrencyRate of a foreign-currency invoice, the partner collective account, or booked VAT/cost detail. If that list returns nothing, fall back to GET /account-histories.info for each AccountHistories[].Id from book-entries.info. Optionally call GET /documents.info with Document.Id for the file name. If the read-back fails, never re-create the invoice: return the Id with echoed values.
  12. Assemble InvoiceItemOutMultiAnalyticPlans from the request echo, the resolved Ids and the read-back values (see the response mappings). Currency rule (D4): amounts are the echoed invoice-currency amounts with the echoed currency; accounting_info.main_currency_total = the EUR partner-line amount; EUR amounts are never paired with a foreign currency code.

Considerations and limitations

  • No VAT-code entity or VAT-code list endpoint appears among the 72 documented endpoints. Invoice lines need TaxRate + TaxOrigin + TaxType (+ TaxKind/TaxCondition/TaxConditionValue for purchases), checked against external reference files of accepted combinations. VatCode/VatHeading appear only as read-only output (account-histories VatDetail), where TaxCondition has an extra value '4 = Article' that the purchase input does not accept (enum[0, 1, 2, 3]). Accounts carry VAT settings (VatRate/VatNat) that can help default or validate a decoded combination. Impact: Chift GET vat-codes must be emulated from a connector-maintained catalogue with the canonical D2 id grammar (S-{TaxRate}-{TaxOrigin}-{TaxType}[-{TaxCountryCode}] / P-{TaxRate}-{TaxOrigin}-{TaxType}-{TaxKind}-{TaxCondition}-{TaxConditionValue}), and lines[].tax_code is decoded from it. The catalogue must be maintained whenever Horus changes its accepted combinations, and the combination files are not available for this analysis. Read-side asymmetry: VatDetail has no TaxKind, so Invoice retrieval returns tax_code = null for purchase combinations that differ only by TaxKind (D2); a purchase tax_code written here may not survive the round trip. Derived
  • Invoice Number is numeric (Chift invoice_number is a string). Invoices have no AutoGenerateNumber field, unlike miscellaneous operations, banks and financials. No uniqueness constraint, idempotency key or external reference field is documented for invoices. Impact: Non-numeric invoice numbers are rejected. Auto-numbering for posted sales must be emulated (max+1, with a race risk). Idempotency must be emulated with a book-entries.list DocumentNo pre-check that returns 409. Documented
  • No dedicated field for reference (the supplier's invoice number on purchases). Header free text is limited to Comment and PaymentReferences. Impact: reference is merged into Comment with the D3 convention ('{reference} | {customer_memo}', or '| {customer_memo}' without reference), so later GET invoices cannot filter on it and can only parse it back from the header Comment, whose location on read is undocumented. Comment length limits are undocumented (to confirm). Derived
  • Several Chift inputs have no Horus field: force_financial_period, withholding_tax, nl_payment_terms_split, start_date/end_date, delivery_date, payment_term_id, and line unit_price/quantity/unit_of_measure. Impact: These are either rejected (force_financial_period when it differs from the invoice month, withholding_tax, nl_payment_terms_split) or ignored with a warning (accrual/delivery dates, payment term, quantity/price). Clients relying on them lose that data. Derived
  • Horus analytics are amounts on joint cost-line combinations (CostLines[].Amount + Costs[] per schema), not per-plan percentages. A folder can have at most 8 cost schemas, and each cost history at most 8 cost sections. Schemas can be Mandatory or Free (CostSectionType 900). Impact: Percentages must be converted to amounts with largest-remainder cent rounding (D8). Several split plans require a Cartesian product, which assumes the plans are independent. A read-back cannot reproduce integer percentages exactly. Free schemas (CostSectionType 900) are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 (D8), so their values cannot be posted through the connector. Documented
  • Currency: EUR is implicit (null). Only 32 other ISO codes are accepted. The folder must be configured for currencies, and no API field exposes that setting. The CurrencyRate direction is undocumented, and so is the currency of the invoice amounts. Impact: Invoices in other currencies are rejected. A rate in the wrong direction would misstate EUR amounts, so validate with Horus before go-live. D4 assumption: account-history Debit/Credit and CompanyDetail amounts are in EUR, so the response pairs echoed invoice-currency amounts with the echoed currency and returns the EUR partner-line amount only as main_currency_total. Documented
  • Create returns only {Id} (Guide: Responses & errors). GET /book-entries.info reads the entry back by that Id with no period, but it returns entry-level data only: lines appear as references (AccountHistories: Type + Id), without amounts, VAT, cost detail, currency or account search keys. Line data comes from account-histories.list, which needs FolderId and mandatory period parameters, or from GET /account-histories.info at one call per line. Impact: In the common case, the full Chift response needs echoed values plus 1 read-back call by Id (last_updated_on, status, DocumentNo, header totals, document). The actual EUR totals of a foreign-currency invoice and the partner collective account still need a second, period-bounded call (0-1 extra). Documented
  • invoices.*.update is a full replacement: it needs every creation-required field, returns a NEW book entry Id, and deletes the original attachment. Impact: Chift invoice ids do not survive Horus updates, for example a draft confirmed later through the API. Any later status change or correction means re-sending the full payload and the PDF, and the Chift id and attachments_info change. Clients storing ids must be warned. Horus recommends reconciling on daybook + entry number rather than the Id alone. Documented
  • Credit notes use Type=1, but the sources do not say whether amounts must be positive, or whether a credit note may be posted into a 200/100 daybook (or an invoice into 250/150). The posting guide does not tie Type to a daybook type. The connector applies the D1 fallback (customer_refund 250 → 200, supplier_refund 150 → 100), which relies on that undocumented acceptance. Impact: A wrong sign or a refused daybook gives a Horus 400 or reversed postings. Credit notes posted into a 200/100 daybook can be told apart on read only by the partner-line sign (D1, Invoice retrieval). Needs Horus confirmation before go-live. To confirm
  • A single DueDate per invoice; no instalments. Impact: due_dates[] in the response always has exactly one element. Documented
  • Accepted attachment types are documented for all upload routes, invoices.*.new multipart included: Pdf, Xml, CODA, SODA, .jpg/.jpeg/.png (documented). No size limit is documented. Impact: The connector can reject unsupported types before calling, but it cannot pre-check size. Large files may fail with an undocumented error that must be surfaced. To confirm
  • Confirmed means confirmed by the accountant. API entries are unconfirmed by default precisely so the accountant can review them in Horus Office. The Chift default status is 'posted'. Impact: Mapping posted to Confirmed=true bypasses the fiduciary's review. D12: offer the per-consumer setting 'always push unconfirmed' (recommended where the accountant must review API entries); which default fits fiduciary clients is an open product decision. Documented
  • Horus legislations are BE and LU only, and the TaxOrigin labels are Belgian/Luxembourgish tax categories (e.g. 'Prestation hors Belgique', 'Prestations hors Luxembourg'). Impact: Non-BE/LU tax scenarios cannot be expressed, and NL-specific Chift fields have no meaning here. Derived
  • The API has no invoice delete or cancel endpoint. The only book-entry delete is book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.delete, which works only on unconfirmed miscellaneous operations created through the API. Impact: The connector cannot undo a wrongly pushed or duplicated invoice, or roll back a multi-step flow (e.g. a failed fallback PDF attach); the accountant must fix it in Horus Office. That is why the duplicate pre-check before any retry is critical. The connector can never produce Chift status 'cancelled'. Derived
  • All requests, writes included, run against the license's real database. No sandbox environment is documented. Impact: Connector development, QA and certification must use a dedicated test license or test folder. Because invoices cannot be deleted, test invoices remain. Documented
  • Neither the API reference nor the guides document pagination, rate limits or the ErrorType catalogue; the only ErrorType example is 'not_found'. Impact: Lookup volume (several list calls per folder) and throttling behaviour are unknown, and mapping Horus 400s precisely to Chift 409/422/404 depends on ErrorType values Horus must provide. To confirm
  • partner_info carries only account_number (no description in the Chift spec, read as the company SearchKey, D10) and account_name. Companies created from it have no VAT number, no address, IsVatLiable=false, and the folder's language and country. Impact: A wrong reading of account_number would create or reuse the wrong third party. Companies created this way need completing in Horus Office; calling Create client/supplier first is the reliable path. Assumption
  • Full attribute-level mapping in section 5 (Invoice creation (multiple analytic plans)). Derived
Detailed open questions (25)
  • Horus Can a credit note (Type 1) be posted into a 200/100 daybook, and an invoice into 250/150? For credit notes, must amounts be positive? The D1 auto-selection posts customer_refund into a 200 daybook (supplier_refund into 100) when the folder has no 250 (150) daybook. Neither the API reference nor the posting guide ties Type to a daybook type or states the sign.
  • Horus Is Number unique per daybook and fiscal year? Does invoices.*.new reject or flag a duplicate Number, and with which ErrorType? Is there an idempotency key or external reference for invoices, or a way to get the next free number of an invoice daybook? (AutoGenerateNumber exists only on miscellaneous operations, banks and financials. Documents expose Status '900: PossibleDuplicata' / '901: DuplicataNumber', which hints at duplicate detection on the document side only.) Needed for Chift's invoice_number idempotency and for auto-numbering of posted invoices.
  • Horus Please provide the accepted-combination files (Tax-combinations-sales.xlsx and Tax-combinations-purchases.xlsx). Is there an API endpoint, current or planned, that lists VAT codes or accepted tax combinations? Are the files versioned? The D2 synthetic VAT catalogue behind Chift GET vat-codes and lines[].tax_code is built from these files, which are not available for this analysis.
  • Horus Direction and meaning of CurrencyRate (1 EUR = x CUR, or 1 CUR = x EUR); currency of CompanyDetail amounts (and of account-history Debit/Credit). Are invoices.*.new TotalAmount/BaseAmount/TaxAmount given in CurrencyCode or in EUR? (invoices.sales.outstanding.* return both TotalAmount and TotalCurrencyAmount, which suggests EUR versus invoice currency.) How can we detect that a folder 'allows currencies'? D4 assumes Debit/Credit and CompanyDetail amounts are EUR. Needed to map force_currency_exchange, currency_exchange_rate and the amounts correctly, and to compute main_currency_total.
  • Horus Does Horus derive the accounting period (AccountingMonth/AccountingYear) from Date, and is there any way to force the period on invoice creation? Determines whether force_financial_period can ever be supported, and which period to query on read-back.
  • Horus With several cost schemas, must every CostLine carry one Costs entry per schema, only for Mandatory schemas, or can rows mix schemas? What value format applies to Free schemas (CostSectionType 900)? Are locked (IsLocked) cost sections refused on posting? Validates the percentage-to-joint-combination conversion for the multi-plan route, and decides whether Free schemas (excluded in v1 under D8) can be supported later.
  • Horus What size limit applies to the file in the multipart variant of invoices.sales.new and invoices.purchases.new? File types are documented, but Chift accepts any base64 pdf, and we need to pre-validate size.
  • Horus What are the maximum lengths of Comment, PaymentReferences and the line Comment? Is PaymentReferences validated as a Belgian structured communication? The D3 convention concatenates reference and customer_memo into the header Comment, so truncation rules must be defined.
  • Horus Please confirm that the 'Id' query parameter of GET /invoices.sales.outstanding.info is the FolderId (deduced from the guide, which requires FolderId). Needed if this endpoint is used as a sales read-back. The deduction is strong but not stated.
  • Horus What are the rate limits, and do list endpoints paginate or truncate large results? Neither the API reference nor the guides (Making requests, Responses & errors) document pagination or rate limits, and list responses show no paging fields. Each invoice triggers 2-4 calls when the cache is warm, and lookups rely on list endpoints.
  • Horus GET /book-entries.info documents only the Id parameter: does it really need no FolderId? For a foreign-currency invoice, in which currency are CompanyDetail.TotalAmount/TurnoverAmount/TaxAmount expressed? Is TurnoverAmount the invoice's BaseAmount? What format does CompanyDetail.DueDate use (typed string, unlike Date)? Is sideloading planned for this endpoint? book-entries.info is now the default read-back. These answers decide which response fields it can fill reliably, and whether the period-bounded account-histories.list call can be dropped for foreign-currency invoices.
  • Horus Which ErrorType values can invoices.*.new return (duplicate number, closed period, invalid tax combination, unknown account/cost section, currency not allowed, missing vehicle)? Only the example 'not_found' is documented. Precise mapping to Chift 404/409/422 depends on it.
  • Horus When the accountant confirms or edits an API-created invoice in Horus Office, does the book entry keep its Id? Is an invoice delete/cancel endpoint planned? Chift invoice ids must stay stable, and the API offers no way to roll back an invoice.
  • Horus What happens when VehicleId is missing on a purchase line whose account requires a vehicle, and when companies.new receives a SearchKey that already exists? Chift has no vehicle source, and the partner_info flow can collide on SearchKey. Both behaviours are undocumented.
  • Chift What sign convention does Chift expect on input for customer_refund and supplier_refund amounts? The request schema does not state it, and Horus has a separate Type flag.
  • Chift Is it acceptable to reject non-numeric invoice_number values? When the duplicate pre-check finds a match, should the connector return 409 or the existing invoice? The Chift idempotency guide was not provided.
  • Chift Is a connector-maintained synthetic VAT-code catalogue acceptable for GET vat-codes on a target with no VAT-code entity? It is the only way to satisfy the required lines[].tax_code.
  • Chift For unsupported optional inputs (withholding_tax, nl_payment_terms_split, start/end_date, delivery_date, force_financial_period), should the connector reject them or ignore them with a warning? This is a product policy: it trades silent data loss against integration friction.
  • Chift For analytics, is the Cartesian-product (independence) conversion from per-plan percentages acceptable for multi-plan splits? Horus needs joint combinations; Chift provides only marginal splits per plan.
  • Chift Which default fits fiduciary clients for status 'posted': Confirmed=true, or the per-consumer 'always push unconfirmed' setting switched on by default (D12)? Horus's view on API-confirmed entries would inform it. It is a business decision with an accounting-control impact for fiduciary clients.
  • Chift Is the PaymentMode enum value (as a string) acceptable as the id for GET payment-methods and payment_method_id? (Analytic ids are fixed by D8 as cost schema / cost section GUIDs.) The TRANSFORM rule for payment_method_id and due_dates[].payment_method_id depends on that choice.
  • Horus Can TaxKind be exposed on read (account-histories VatDetail)? What does the read-side TaxCondition value '4 = Article' mean, given that purchase input accepts only enum[0, 1, 2, 3]? Without TaxKind on read, Invoice retrieval cannot re-encode some D2 purchase tax codes written by this route (tax_code returns null), so the create → read round trip loses the VAT code.
  • Horus Where does the invoice header Comment surface on read (partner-line Comment, RemarkCompany, Remark)? reference and customer_memo are stored in the header Comment (D3); Invoice retrieval and outstandings can only return them if they know where to read it back.
  • Horus Does invoices.*.new refuse a CompanyId whose IsCustomer (sales) or IsSupplier (purchases) flag is false, or a CompanyId from another folder? Does companies.new accept LanguageCode 'XX'? Decides whether the D10 companies.update flag step and the D13 companies.info folder guard are mandatory or defensive, and whether the D9 folder-language fallback can fail.
  • Chift What does partner_info.account_number mean: a third-party code or a ledger account? The Chift spec gives no description. The connector treats it as the Horus company SearchKey (D10); if it is the collective ledger account (e.g. 400000), the find-or-create logic is wrong.
Journal retrieval and accounting folders retrievalSupported with workaround SupportedGET …/accounting/journals · GET …/accounting/foldersFolders map almost one to one onto folders.list. Journals come from daybooks.list, with the counterpart account looked up in the chart of accounts and next document numbers only emulated.

Folders map almost one to one onto folders.list. Journals come from daybooks.list: the daybook type maps cleanly to Chift's journal type, the counterpart account needs a lookup in the cached chart of accounts, and Chift fields that Horus does not hold (blocked, unallocated account, next document numbers) are derived or emulated.

Detailed analysis

Horus vocabulary maps onto Chift's. A Horus license (one fiduciary or SMB database with its own api_url) is one Chift consumer connection. A Horus folder ('the accounting record of one company') is the Chift folder, a Horus daybook is a Chift journal, and a Horus fiscal year is a Chift bookyear. Watch out: a Horus 'company' is a third party (customer, supplier or beneficiary), not the legal entity. Get Folders is close to 1:1 with POST /folders.list (Mode 0). GET /folders.info adds the country as optional enrichment, and active, main_currency (EUR) and company_number are constants or derived. Get journals needs several calls. POST /daybooks.list gives the core fields. The cached chart of accounts (accounts.list) turns ConcentrationAccount.Id into an account number. Only if Chift needs next_document_numbers: fiscal-years.list, then either one book-entries.list per daybook × fiscal year, or one account-histories.list per fiscal year grouped by DaybookSearchKey, because Horus exposes no numbering sequence. The daybook Type maps onto Chift's journal_type through the shared D6 table used by every route. Types with a documented posting endpoint (100/150/200/250/500/590/600) map by deduction (derived). 550/610/690/700 and 0/900/990 are mapped and blocked on assumptions (assumption). Horus has distinct credit-note daybook types (150/250), but invoices carry their own Type (0/1), so credit notes may also sit in 200/100 daybooks: journal_type describes the daybook, not every entry in it (D1). Type is also the routing key for every write route, because it decides which posting endpoint is used. folder_id follows the shared D7 rule: it is required when more than one folder is selected; otherwise the connector uses the single selected folder, or the single folder from folders.list Mode 0; a Horus 403 becomes a Chift 400. Every Id-only .info call is checked against the folder (D13). Main risks: no native blocked, unallocated or currency-permission data; an undocumented numbering format; IsClosed documented only on fiscal-years.info; and Mode 0 can return a fiduciary's whole portfolio. On parameters: FolderId goes in the JSON body of nearly every folder-scoped .list/.new call. Updates and .info reads identify the entity by its own Id; .info is a GET with ?Id=, except POST cost-schemas.info and POST cost-sections.info, which take the Id in the body.

Horus endpoints

  • PrimaryPOST /daybooks.list Primary source for Get journals: all daybooks of a folder with Id, SearchKey, Heading, Type, Iban, Bic, WithCurrency, CurrencyCode, IsUsed, IsWages, ConcentrationAccount, Folder. Type feeds the shared D6 journal_type/blocked table. The same cached list serves the write routes' daybook auto-selection: the D1 per-invoice_type filter with refund fallback for Invoice creation, and Type 600 only for Miscellaneous operation creation (D6) (1 per Chift call (reference data: cache per folder))
  • AlternativeGET /daybooks.info Single daybook by Id. Not needed for the list. Used to validate a journal_id passed to write routes: Data.Type must fit the route (D1/D6) and Data.Folder.Id must equal the resolved folder_id, otherwise 400 (D13) (1 per journal_id validated (optional))
  • LookupPOST /accounts.list Turns daybook ConcentrationAccount {Type: Accounts, Id} into the account number (SearchKey) for counterpart_account (cached reference data (1 per folder, reused across routes))
  • AlternativeGET /accounts.info Fallback when a ConcentrationAccount.Id is not in the cached chart (accounts.list documents no IncludeInactive filter), or an alternative to loading the whole chart: resolve one Id → SearchKey. Id-only call, so check Data.Folder.Id = folder_id, otherwise 400 (D13) (1 per distinct unresolved ConcentrationAccount (Bank/Financial daybooks only))
  • EnrichmentPOST /fiscal-years.list Primary source for Get bookyears. Bookyear frame (Heading, StartDate, EndDate) for journals.next_document_numbers. Closure dates for write-route pre-checks. Filter.Dates [entryDate] resolves the fiscal year that contains a given date (write routes) (1 per folder (cache))
  • Follow-upGET /fiscal-years.info Only documented source of IsClosed: needed for Chift bookyears.closed. Optionally reused (from the same cache) to include past but still-open years in the next_document_numbers emulation. Id-only call, so check Data.Folder.Id = folder_id (D13) (1 per fiscal year (N+1, cacheable, shared between Get bookyears and Get journals))
  • OptionalPOST /book-entries.list Optional emulation of next_document_number: highest DocumentNo per daybook within a fiscal year's period. The write routes use the same endpoint with Filter.DocumentNo for the D12 duplicate pre-check (1 per postable daybook × selected fiscal year (expensive; opt-in only))
  • AlternativePOST /account-histories.list Cheaper-in-calls alternative for the next_document_numbers emulation: one call per fiscal year returns every entry line of the folder across all daybooks. Group by DaybookSearchKey and take max(DocumentNo), ignoring 0. Bigger payload (one row per line), so use gzip (1 per selected fiscal year (instead of 1 per daybook × fiscal year))
  • PrimaryPOST /folders.list Primary source for Get Folders: folders visible to the authenticated user. Also the D7 default-folder fallback: when folder_id is omitted and no activation selection is stored, a Mode 0 result with exactly one folder gives the folder to use (1 per Chift call (plus the D7 fallback call when no selection is stored; cacheable))
  • EnrichmentGET /folders.info Enrichment: CountryCode, which only .info returns, for addresses[].country. Also the natural mapping for Chift 'Get Folder' by id (1 per folder (N+1, cache; optional))
  • OptionalGET /users.me At activation: IsAccountant tells whether the user is an accountant (Horus Office) or an entrepreneur (Horus Mobile), which changes what folders.list Mode returns. Id can be compared with folders ResponsibleUser.Id to flag the accountant's own folders (once per connection)
  • OptionalGET /licenses.me Connection metadata (license name, language) for labelling the Chift consumer connection. No FolderItem field (once per connection)
  • OptionalGET /licenses.main Connection metadata: the fiduciary/SMB license that owns the database. No FolderItem field (once per connection)

Call sequence

  1. Activation (once per Chift consumer, i.e. one Horus license connection with its own api_url and tokens): GET /users.me → IsAccountant (persona) and Id. Optional: GET /licenses.me and GET /licenses.main to label the connection.
  2. Get Folders 1: POST /folders.list {"Mode": 0} → Data[] (Id, FolderName, IsVatLiable, VatNumber, Address, ZipCode, Locality, Legislation, ResponsibleUser, …).
  3. Get Folders 2 (optional N+1, cache): GET /folders.info?Id={Data[].Id} → CountryCode for addresses[].country (folders.list has no CountryCode).
  4. Get Folders 3: merge with Chift's activation selection (selected), stored per consumer because it drives the D7 folder_id default. active = true for every folder returned. A previously selected folder that is no longer returned → active = false. Optional (derived): in the activation picker, mark folders where ResponsibleUser.Id = users.me Id as 'mine'. For accountants this is equivalent to Mode 1, without a second call.
  5. Get journals 0: resolve the folder with the shared D7 rule. If the Chift query folder_id is given, use it as the Horus FolderId (GUID). If it is omitted: exactly one folder selected at activation → use it; no selection stored → POST /folders.list {"Mode": 0} and use its folder if exactly one is returned; otherwise 400 'folder_id required'. A Horus 403 on any folder-scoped call below → Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' (D7).
  6. Get journals 1: POST /daybooks.list {"FolderId": …} with no Filter, so every Type comes back → Data[].
  7. Get journals 2: POST /accounts.list {"FolderId": …} (cached per folder) → map Id → SearchKey. Resolve ConcentrationAccount.Id of Bank/Financial daybooks → counterpart_account. If an Id is missing from the cache, fall back to GET /accounts.info?Id= and check Data.Folder.Id = FolderId (D13). daybooks.list does not support sideloading.
  8. Get journals 3 (optional, opt-in): POST /fiscal-years.list {"FolderId": …, "Filter": {"IncludeFuture": true}} (cached). Select the fiscal years: by default those with EndDate >= today (current and future; proxy for 'open', A). Optionally also past years whose IsClosed = false, read from the fiscal-years.info cache shared with Get bookyears (adds N .info calls if not cached). For each selected year, either (a) POST /book-entries.list {FolderId, DaybookId, StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear from the year} per postable daybook, or (b) one POST /account-histories.list {FolderId, period, Filter.IncludeReport: false} grouped by DaybookSearchKey. next_document_number = max(DocumentNo, ignoring 0) + 1.
  9. Get journals 4: map Type → journal_type and blocked with the shared D6 table (blocked = true for 550, 610, 690, 700, 900, 990, 0 and unknown Types, A), derive other_currencies_allowed from Type, slice page/size in memory, and return ChiftPage with total = number of mapped daybooks.
  10. Get bookyears (context): resolve the folder with the same D7 rule. POST /fiscal-years.list {FolderId, Filter.IncludeFuture: true}, then GET /fiscal-years.info?Id= per fiscal year for IsClosed (N+1, cache; check Data.Folder.Id = FolderId, D13). First test whether the list response actually includes IsClosed (the Data model guide implies it); if it does, drop the N+1.

Considerations and limitations

  • Horus has no 'next document number' per daybook or fiscal year. Invoice endpoints have no AutoGenerateNumber; only bank, financial and MO endpoints do, and only when Confirmed = true. Impact: next_document_numbers is either [] or an approximate emulation: one book-entries.list per daybook × fiscal year, or one heavier account-histories.list per fiscal year; the format is undocumented. Mitigation: post unconfirmed entries with Number 0, which the docs allow, and let the accountant number them. Under D12 this mitigation covers Chift 'draft' and consumers with the 'always push unconfirmed' setting only. Chift 'posted' (the default) sends Confirmed = true, so a confirmed invoice needs a real Number: either from invoice_number or from the same max(DocumentNo)+1 emulation (assumption). It also needs a duplicate pre-check with book-entries.list Filter.DocumentNo (D12). Documented
  • No unallocated or suspense account is exposed on daybooks or folders (verified absence). Impact: unallocated_account is always null, so bank reconciliation flows that rely on it cannot be prefilled. Derived
  • Daybooks have no blocked, active or archived flag. IsUsed exists, but its meaning is undocumented (to confirm). Impact: blocked is derived from Type with the shared D6 table: true for every type without a documented posting endpoint (550, 610, 690, 700, 900, 990, 0; A). A daybook disabled in Horus Office would still look postable. Derived
  • Some daybook attributes can be written but not read. BankName, UsedForSupplierPayments and UsedForClientDomiciliations exist on daybooks.new but are not returned by daybooks.list/info. Impact: They cannot be used to qualify bank journals, and IsUsed should not be assumed to be one of them. Documented
  • The folder-level 'allow currencies' configuration is not readable through the API. Impact: other_currencies_allowed for sales/purchase journals is an assumption, so a foreign-currency invoice can still fail with a 400 at posting time. Derived
  • Horus lists are not paginated, and daybooks.list has no ModifiedAfter filter. Impact: page and size are emulated in memory, and change detection means a full reload. Acceptable because daybooks are few and are cached reference data. Derived
  • Folders carry no status, enterprise number or currency, and CountryCode is only on folders.info. Impact: active and main_currency are constants. company_number is derived for BE only. addresses[].country costs N+1 calls or an approximation. Documented
  • folders.list Mode semantics depend on the persona. For an accountant, Mode 0 returns every folder of the fiduciary license. Impact: The activation picker can list hundreds of folders, which needs search or filtering in the Chift UI. Access rights are enforced by Horus ('What a user can see through the API depends on their access in Horus'). Documented
  • fiscal-years.list does not return IsClosed in the reference; only fiscal-years.info does. The Data model guide nonetheless lists IsClosed among fiscal-year attributes. Impact: Get bookyears costs 1 + N calls (cache them). The next_document_numbers emulation cannot filter on 'open' years from the list alone, so it uses an EndDate proxy or the shared .info cache. Documented
  • Separate credit-note daybook types exist (150/250), but invoices also carry Type 0/1, and the guide allows 200/250 or 100/150 daybooks without saying which Type each accepts. Impact: journal_type describes the daybook, not every entry in it: credit notes may sit in 200/100 daybooks (D1). A folder may have no 250/150 daybook. Its customer_refund/supplier_refund journal list is then empty, and Invoice creation falls back to the 200/100 daybook with Type = 1 (D1). Read routes therefore scan both sibling types and classify entries by partner-line sign (D1). To be validated with Horus. Assumption
  • One Horus license = one api_url and one token pair, so an integration needs one connection per license. Impact: A Chift consumer covers the folders of a single license. A firm or user with several licenses needs several consumers. Documented
