Detailed open questions (16)
  • Horus What does daybooks IsUsed mean (active, has entries, used for supplier payments?), and can a daybook be deactivated or blocked in Horus Office? Determines whether IsUsed can drive Chift 'blocked', or whether unused daybooks should be hidden.
  • Horus Can a credit note (invoices.sales/purchases.new Type = 1) be posted into a Sale (200) or Purchase (100) daybook, and can an invoice (Type 0) go into a 250/150 daybook? Must refunds go to a credit-note daybook when one exists? (D1) Validates the D1 refund fallback (250 → 200, 150 → 100) and confirms that journal_type describes the daybook, not every entry in it.
  • Horus Can the document Type (0 invoice / 1 credit note) be exposed on read (book-entries.list/info or account-histories.list)? (D1) Credit notes may sit in 200/100 daybooks, so journal_type cannot classify entries. D1 relies on the partner-line sign (assumption) until the Type is readable.
  • Horus Is there a way to read the next document number per daybook and fiscal year, and what is the DocumentNo/Number format (e.g. YYMM + sequence)? Do unconfirmed entries with a Number reserve that number? Could AutoGenerateNumber be added to invoices.*.new? next_document_numbers cannot be emulated reliably, and confirmed invoices need a valid Number.
  • Horus Are StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear in book-entries.list and account-histories.list calendar months or accounting periods? Are closing/opening entries (EntryType 810/850/910) dated inside the fiscal year bounds? What does IncludeReport cover? Correct period bounds for the next_document_numbers emulation and for ledger reads.
  • Horus How can an integrator read whether a folder is 'configured to allow currencies', and is a folder's base currency always EUR? Needed for other_currencies_allowed and main_currency.
  • Horus Does folders.list return archived or closed folders? What does IsPrivate mean? Is the folder's enterprise number (BCE/KBO) available anywhere? Needed for FolderItem.active and company_number, and to decide whether IsPrivate folders should be offered at activation.
  • Horus Which HTTP status does Horus return for a FolderId the user cannot access, or for a deleted folder: 403, 404 or 400 ('references non-existing resources')? (D7) D7 maps Horus 403 to Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection'. Any other code Horus uses for the same situation needs the same mapping.
  • Horus Can Cash (550) or TemporaryMiscOperation (610) daybooks receive entries through book-entries.financials.new or miscellaneous-operations.new? What about 690/700, and is CostMiscOperation (700) a kind of miscellaneous operation? (D6) Decides blocked and journal_type for these types in the shared D6 table. They are currently blocked = true and mapped on assumptions; both endpoints leave DaybookId type unrestricted.
  • Horus Is a suspense or unallocated account configured per bank or financial daybook in Horus Office, and can it be exposed? Chift unallocated_account.
  • Horus Does fiscal-years.list actually return IsClosed (the Data model guide lists it as a fiscal-year attribute)? If not, could it be added? Removes the N+1 calls on Get bookyears and lets the numbering emulation filter open years.
  • Horus Are new daybook Type enum values considered a backwards-compatible change that can appear without notice? Decides whether tolerant mapping of unknown Types is a must-have.
  • Chift For journals that cannot be posted to through the API, the connector lists them with blocked = true, and with journal_type unknown for 0/900/990 (D6). Is that the agreed convention, or should they be hidden? Consistency with other connectors, and the effect on 'auto-select the only journal' logic.
  • Chift Is next_document_numbers used by any Chift logic or client flow? If not, can the Horus connector return [] by default? The emulation costs one call per daybook × fiscal year (or one heavy ledger call per year). Under D12, posted invoices are sent confirmed, so the same max(DocumentNo)+1 logic may still be needed on the write side.
  • Chift Should the Horus connector always enable the multiple-folders feature, and should the activation flow let the user pick folders.list Mode (0 vs 1) or flag 'my folders'? Fiduciary licenses hold many folders, and Mode changes the list for accountants.
  • Chift Confirm that FolderItem.main_currency should be ISO 4217 ('EUR'). The spec says ISO 3166-1. Doc inconsistency; AccountingInfoOut.main_currency says ISO-4217.
Ledger account creation and chart of accounts retrievalSupported with workaround Supported with workaroundPOST …/accounting/accounts · GET …/accounting/chart-of-accountsaccounts.new and accounts.list are native, but the Chift account type cannot be written and must be computed on read; type filtering and pagination are emulated.

Accounts are created with accounts.new (number and name only) after a duplicate check, and read back. The chart of accounts comes from accounts.list, cached per folder. Horus has no writable account type, so the Chift type cannot be set on creation and is computed on read from daybooks, categories and, as a last resort, the account number. Type filtering and pagination are emulated.

Detailed analysis

Horus has its own Accounts resource: the folder's general chart of accounts. Each account has a GUID Id and a SearchKey that is unique in the folder and 'usually the account number'. Create ledger account therefore maps to POST /accounts.new (FolderId, SearchKey ← number, Heading ← name). An exact-match duplicate check on accounts.list runs before it. A GET /accounts.info read-back runs after it, because .new returns only the Id, and the read-back also checks that Data.Folder.Id is the requested folder (D13). The Chift type cannot be written (accounts.new and accounts.update have no category field), so the type returned is computed from Horus and can differ from the request. Get chart of accounts maps to POST /accounts.list, best fetched in full and cached per folder as the guide recommends. classes is applied as a 'starts with' prefix, and the type filter, page/size and total are emulated in the connector, since Horus has no category filter and no pagination. Every call is scoped by FolderId, resolved with the shared folder rule (D7):

  • folder_id is required when the connection has more than one selected folder;
  • the single folder selected at activation is the default;
  • with no stored selection, a folder is used only if POST /folders.list {Mode: 0} returns exactly one;
  • otherwise the connector returns 400 'folder_id required', and a Horus 403 becomes a Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection'.

Chift type has no account-level Horus field and is computed from several sources, in order:

  • daybooks.list concentration accounts (bank, financial; cash is uncertain);
  • IntCategory (only 'COLLECCUS' is documented);
  • optionally, company concentration accounts and line-level AccountType ('100' / '200' / '400' / '500' on account-history or outstanding lines, which only covers accounts with movements);
  • AccountCategory / AccountSubCategory;
  • finally, a Belgian number-prefix fallback (an assumption).

Customers and suppliers are Horus 'companies', usually booked on shared concentration accounts (e.g. 400000/440000) with a CompanyId. The chart therefore normally holds general and collective accounts and no per-partner accounts. scheme_ids is always null. The main risks are:

  • type reliability (payable, vat, cash);
  • undocumented SearchKey rules and duplicate behaviour;
  • no delete or renumber endpoint for accounts anywhere in the 72-endpoint reference.

Under the shared verdict calibration (D11), both routes are 'workaround'.

Horus endpoints

  • PrimaryPOST /accounts.new Creates the general ledger account (SearchKey + Heading) in the folder's chart of accounts. (1 per Chift create call)
  • Follow-upGET /accounts.info Reads the created account back to build the Chift AccountItem response (active, type, accounting_category), because accounts.new returns only the Id. Cross-folder guard (D13): Data.Folder.Id must equal the resolved FolderId, otherwise return a Chift 400. (1 per Chift create call (chained on the returned Id))
  • PrimaryPOST /accounts.list Chart: source list of the folder's accounts, fetched in full (no Filter) and cached per folder. classes is applied in the connector, or sent as SearchKeys when not caching. Create: duplicate check on number before accounts.new. (Chart: 1 per folder per cache refresh, returns the full list with no paging. Create: 1 per call (fresh, uncached) for the duplicate check.)
  • EnrichmentPOST /daybooks.list Enrichment for type: the ConcentrationAccount of Bank (500) and Financial (590) daybooks identifies bank and other_financial accounts. For Cash (550) daybooks it is uncertain (to confirm) whether a ConcentrationAccount is exposed. Daybook Type codes are read with the shared daybook Type table (D6). Here they type the concentration account, not the journal (journal_type belongs to Get journals). (cached reference data, 1 per folder (reused by both routes))
  • OptionalPOST /companies.list Optional enrichment for type: collects the CustomerConcentrationAccount Ids (receivable) and SupplierConcentrationAccount Ids (payable) used by the folder's companies. (cached reference data, 1 per folder (can be large; ModifiedAfter allows incremental refresh))
  • OptionalPOST /account-histories.list Optional enrichment for type: entry lines carry a line-level AccountType with AccountSearchKey and Account {Type, Id}. Build the map of distinct accounts per AccountType: 100 → receivable, 200 → payable, 400 → vat, 500 → cash (to confirm). This is the only documented VAT marker tied to an account. It only covers accounts with movements in the chosen period. (not called by default; at most 1 per folder per cache refresh (period-bound, potentially large; refresh with ModifiedAfter))
  • OptionalPOST /customers.outstanding Optional, lighter enrichment for type: distinct AccountSearchKey of open customer items (AccountType '100: CollectCust / Customer') → receivable. Only accounts with open items are found. (cached per folder (1 call without CompanyIds filter))
  • OptionalPOST /suppliers.outstanding Optional, lighter enrichment for type: distinct AccountSearchKey of open supplier items (AccountType '200: CollectSup / Supplier') → payable. Only accounts with open items are found. (cached per folder (1 call without CompanyIds filter))
  • LookupPOST /folders.list Matches the Chift folder_id to a Horus FolderId, which every accounts.* call needs, and applies the shared folder rule (D7). When folder_id is missing and no activation selection is stored, use the folder only if Mode 0 returns exactly one; otherwise return 400 'folder_id required'. Folder visibility depends on the connected user's access, and a Horus 403 maps to Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection'. (cached per connection: at connection setup (folder selection), and per D7 as the fallback when folder_id is missing and no activation selection is stored)
  • OptionalGET /folders.info Optional: reads the folder Legislation (BE/LU) to pick the right number-prefix fallback table and any number validation, plus AlternateLanguage. (cached, 1 per folder)
  • AlternativePOST /account-balance.list Not recommended for these routes. It is a trial balance: period-bound, with no Inactive or category fields. Its only extra here is showing that 'titles' exist in the chart. (not used)
  • AlternativePOST /accounts.update Not used by these two routes. It cannot set SearchKey or any category, so it cannot persist the Chift type after creation. (not used)
  • AlternativePOST /daybooks.new Rejected alternative for honouring type=bank or other_financial on create: a Bank (500) or Financial (590) daybook whose ConcentrationAccountId points at the new account would make the account read back as bank or other_financial. Not recommended: it creates a journal (IBAN, bank name...) as a side effect, which the Chift call did not ask for. (not used)
  • AlternativePOST /cost-sections.list Not used (terminology trap). Cost sections are values of an analytical dimension (a cost schema), not general ledger accounts, so they must not feed the chart of accounts. They are also distinct from the analytic AccountCategory codes 1200/1300 found in the general chart. (not used)

Call sequence

  1. CREATE 0 [cached, connection setup] Resolve FolderId with the shared folder rule (D7): - folder_id given: use it; - else exactly one folder selected at activation: use that folder; - else no selection stored: POST /folders.list {Mode: 0}, and use the folder only if exactly one is returned; - otherwise return 400 'folder_id required'. A Horus 403 on any folder-scoped call → Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection'. Optionally GET /folders.info?Id=... to read Legislation (BE/LU).
  2. CREATE 1 POST /accounts.list {FolderId, Filter: {SearchKeys: [number]}}. Make this a fresh call, not the cache. The filter is 'starts with', so keep only Data[] where SearchKey == number exactly. If a match exists, return a Chift 400 'account number already exists' and do not call accounts.new (Horus duplicate behaviour is undocumented). Caveat (to confirm): it is undocumented whether accounts.list returns inactive accounts; if it does not, an inactive duplicate escapes this check.
  3. CREATE 2 (optional, A) Validate in the connector: number against the folder Legislation, and requested type against the number prefix. Horus does not document any SearchKey rules.
  4. CREATE 3 POST /accounts.new {FolderId, SearchKey: number, Heading: name}. type is not sent, because Horus has no field for it. Response: {Id}. - Horus 400: re-run the exact-match check of CREATE 1 (a concurrent creation may have happened) and return a Chift 400 with Horus Details. - Horus 403: return a Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' (D7), with Horus Details when present. Do not retry. - Timeout or Horus 500: never blind-retry, because no idempotency key is documented (same principle as D12). Re-run the exact-match check of CREATE 1 first. If the account now exists with Heading == name, continue to CREATE 4 with its Id. If it exists with another Heading, return a Chift 400 'account number already exists'. If it is absent, retry later with backoff.
  5. CREATE 4 GET /accounts.info?Id={Id}. Cross-folder guard (D13): Data.Folder.Id must equal FolderId, otherwise return a Chift 400 without an AccountItem. Map SearchKey → number, Heading → name, NOT Inactive → active, and AccountCategory → accounting_category. Compute type with the same cascade as the chart, using the cached daybooks.list map. The line-level AccountType step cannot match a brand-new account, which has no movements.
  6. CREATE 5 Invalidate the folder's cached chart of accounts (accounts.list cache and computed types) so the new account appears on the next GET.
  7. CHART 0 Resolve FolderId with the shared folder rule (D7), as in CREATE 0: folder_id, else the single activation folder, else the single folder from folders.list Mode 0, else 400 'folder_id required'. A Horus 403 → Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection'. The resolved FolderId is the cache key for every per-folder cache below.
  8. CHART 1 [cached per folder] POST /accounts.list {FolderId} with Filter omitted ('omitted or null filters are ignored') and gzip enabled. This returns the full chart; Horus has no paging. Alternative when not caching: send classes.split(',') trimmed as Filter.SearchKeys. Never fill the shared cache from a classes-filtered call.
  9. CHART 2 [cached per folder] POST /daybooks.list {FolderId, Filter: {Types: [500, 550, 590]}}: build a map ConcentrationAccount.Id → bank (500) / other_financial (590) / cash (550, ?: may be null for cash daybooks). There is no sideloading on daybooks.list, so the join to accounts.list Data[].Id is done in the connector.
  10. CHART 3 (optional) [cached per folder] POST /companies.list {FolderId}: build the sets of CustomerConcentrationAccount.Id (receivable) and SupplierConcentrationAccount.Id (payable).
  11. CHART 4 (optional) [cached per folder] Line-level AccountType map: either POST /customers.outstanding {FolderId} and POST /suppliers.outstanding {FolderId} (light, open items only), or POST /account-histories.list {FolderId, StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear over a recent window (assumption), Filter: {AccountSearchKeys: ['4','5'] (assumption, BE classes)}} (heavier, refresh with ModifiedAfter). Keep the distinct AccountSearchKey per AccountType: 100 receivable, 200 payable, 400 vat, 500 cash (to confirm).
  12. CHART 5 In the connector, for each account in the cached list: apply the classes filter (SearchKey starts with any trimmed class), compute number, name, active, type (cascade) and accounting_category, then apply the Chift type filter if one was given.
  13. CHART 6 In the connector, sort by SearchKey, set total = count after filtering, return items[(page-1)*size : page*size] and echo page and size.

Considerations and limitations

  • The Chift type cannot be written. The documented accounts.new and accounts.update bodies accept no category, subcategory or IntCategory. Impact: The type sent on create is lost. The type in the response (and in later chart reads) is computed from Horus and can differ. For example, a type=bank account comes back as 'other' or as the prefix guess until a bank daybook uses it as concentration account. Documented
  • type has no account-level Horus field. It must be computed from several fields and endpoints: daybooks.list concentration accounts, IntCategory, optional company concentration accounts, optional line-level AccountType, and AccountCategory/SubCategory, with a number-prefix fallback. Only 'COLLECCUS' is documented for IntCategory, and no account field marks VAT or payable accounts. The line-level AccountType ('200: CollectSup / Supplier', '400: VAT', '500: Cash' on account-history and outstanding lines) does mark them, but only for accounts with movements. Impact: Extra cached calls plus heuristics. Accounts without movements (new or unused VAT, supplier and cash accounts) still depend on the prefix fallback (assumption). The cash and bank split remains uncertain. Derived
  • accounts.list has no pagination and no category filter. Impact: Each Chift page needs the full list (cache per folder, as the guide recommends). The type filter, total and paging are emulated in the connector; performance is fine for normal chart sizes. Derived
  • accounts.list has no ModifiedAfter / incremental filter: the documented Filter has only SearchKeys and Heading. Impact: No delta sync of the chart. Reload fully on cache expiry, and invalidate the cache on create. Derived
  • Sideloading is not supported on accounts.list or daybooks.list. Impact: Relationship objects {Type:'Accounts', Id} (daybook and company concentration accounts) are joined to accounts.list Data[].Id in the connector. The join works on any subset of accounts, but a reusable cache must hold the full, unfiltered chart. Documented
  • accounts.new returns only the Id. Impact: An extra GET /accounts.info is needed to return a complete AccountItem. Documented
  • SearchKey cannot be changed through the API (accounts.update has no SearchKey). None of the 72 endpoints in the Horus reference is an accounts.delete; the only delete endpoints are ibans.delete, book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.delete and library.documents.delete. Impact: A wrongly numbered account created through Chift cannot be renumbered or removed through the API. Validate before creating, and use a test folder while testing. Derived
  • Horus SearchKey filters on accounts.list use 'starts with', not equality. Impact: Duplicate checks and number lookups must compare for exact equality in the connector (e.g. a search for 4000 also returns 400000). Documented
  • No Horus equivalent for scheme_ids. Impact: Always null. Derived
  • Several Horus behaviours are not documented: SearchKey format, length and chart rules (BE/LU), what happens on a duplicate, and whether accounts.list returns inactive accounts. Impact: Errors surface only as Horus 400 responses (use Details). Any connector-side validation is an assumption to confirm with Horus. The duplicate check may miss inactive accounts. To confirm
  • Horus 403 is generic: 'the user/integrator is not allowed to access this resource'. The shared folder rule (D7) maps every Horus 403 to Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection'. The documentation does not say whether a 403 on accounts.new can also mean that the user sees the folder but may not create accounts. Impact: On a write refused for lack of rights, the Chift message can point at folder access when the real cause is a missing right. Pass Horus Details along when a body is returned. The guide says 'Except for authorization errors, errors return an object with three fields', so a body may be missing. Derived

Detailed open questions (17)
  • Horus What is the full list of IntCategory values (only 'COLLECCUS' is documented)? In particular, which codes mark the supplier collective, VAT recoverable/payable, bank and cash accounts? IntCategory would be the most reliable account-level source for receivable, payable, vat, bank and cash. Without it, accounts without movements fall back to number-prefix heuristics.
  • Horus Is the line-level AccountType on account-history lines derived from the account itself (so stable for a given account), and does '500: Cash' cover all treasury accounts (bank included) or only cash accounts? Decides whether AccountType can be used as an account-level type marker, and how to split bank and cash.
  • Horus Do Cash (550) daybooks expose a ConcentrationAccount in daybooks.list? daybooks.new cannot create type 550 and documents ConcentrationAccountId only for Bank and Financial. Decides whether cash accounts can be typed from daybooks.
  • Horus Does accounts.list include inactive accounts by default? It has no IncludeInactive filter, unlike cost-sections.list. The duplicate check and the completeness of the chart (active=false items) depend on it.
  • Horus Does accounts.new set AccountCategory/AccountSubCategory/IntCategory automatically from the SearchKey (e.g. a PCMN template), and can a category be set through the API at all? Decides whether the Chift type can be honoured on create, and what type a freshly created account will report.
  • Horus What are the SearchKey rules (numeric only? fixed length such as 6 digits? allowed classes per Legislation BE/LU?), and what error (ErrorType) is returned on a duplicate? To validate before creating (accounts cannot be renumbered or deleted through the API) and to translate errors cleanly.
  • Horus Are several values in accounts.list Filter.SearchKeys OR-ed? Needed to pass the Chift classes filter (e.g. '6,7') to Horus in a single call when not caching.
  • Horus What does IsSummary mean, and does accounts.list return non-postable 'title' accounts (as account-balance.list does with IncludeTitle)? Affects which items appear in the Chift chart and whether they should be reported as active.
  • Horus Does accounts.list have any size limit, implicit paging or rate limit? The connector fetches the full list per folder (or caches it).
  • Horus What do VatRate and VatNat on an account mean? They may help identify VAT accounts or default VAT settings; they are undocumented.
  • Horus How common are dedicated concentration accounts per partner or partner group (companies.new lets CustomerConcentrationAccount/SupplierConcentrationAccount be set explicitly), and is a per-partner account model ever used? Confirms whether the chart can contain partner-specific collective accounts, and how client/supplier account_number should be filled.
  • Horus Does a 403 on accounts.new or accounts.list distinguish 'no access to the folder' from 'not allowed to create or read accounts' (for example through ErrorType), and does a 403 response carry an error body at all? The shared folder rule (D7) maps every Horus 403 to Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection'. If the user can see the folder but lacks the create right, that message misleads the client.
  • Chift What exactly do other_expense and other_financial mean, and should ADispo/AInvest/EDepreciation/EOther map to them? The Horus subcategories need a clear target. other_expense and other_financial have no description in the Chift spec.
  • Chift When the requested type cannot be persisted, should the connector ignore it silently, warn, or reject? Should type=bank ever trigger the creation of a Bank daybook (daybooks.new with ConcentrationAccountId)? Create would otherwise return a type that differs from the request. Creating a daybook is a side effect the client did not ask for.
  • Chift Is null acceptable for scheme_ids, and should accounting_category hold the Horus enum name, the numeric code, or category/subcategory combined? To keep the format consistent across connectors.
  • Chift Should analytic accounts (CostInc/CostExp) and title accounts be excluded from the chart of accounts? Unified-model scope decision.
  • Chift Is the extra cost of the optional type enrichment (account-histories.list or the outstanding endpoints) acceptable, or should type rely on reference data and prefixes only? It improves payable, vat and cash typing, but adds period-bound calls with no Chift parameter to scope them.
Miscellaneous operation creationSupported with workaroundPOST /consumers/{consumer_id}/accounting/miscellaneous-operationNative creation of a balanced debit/credit operation. EUR only and numeric numbers only; the entry is read back after creation.

Horus has a dedicated endpoint that posts a balanced debit/credit operation in a miscellaneous daybook. The connector splits Chift's signed amounts into debit and credit, maps line types (customer, supplier, general) and resolves accounts, partners and cost sections. It is a workaround because Horus operations have no currency field (EUR only), numbers must be numeric, and a read-back is needed when Horus generates the number.

Detailed analysis

Chift 'Create miscellaneous operation' maps onto the dedicated Horus POST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.new ("Insert a miscellaneous operation (OD). The result will contain the generated book entry's id."), posted as application/json into a daybook of Type 600 (MiscOperation), the only daybook type this route auto-selects or accepts (D6). The payload needs only deterministic transformations: each signed Chift amount becomes the non-negative Debit/Credit columns (positive = Credit), the line type becomes Type 1/2/3, partner_id becomes CompanyId on customer/supplier lines, status becomes Confirmed (posted → true unless the per-consumer 'always push unconfirmed' setting is on, D12) plus AutoGenerateNumber when a confirmed operation has no number, the date gets a time and offset, and analytic_account (a cost-section GUID) becomes one CostLine whose schema and section search keys come from the per-folder analytics cache (D8). The call pattern is: resolve the folder (D7) and the cached reference data (daybooks.list; cost-schemas.list + cost-sections.list with IncludeInactive=true; optional accounts/companies checks with Folder.Id guards, D13); when operation_number is supplied, get the fiscal-year period from fiscal-years.list and run a book-entries.list Filter.DocumentNo duplicate pre-check (D12); post; then, only when Horus generated the number or the stored state must be confirmed, read back with GET /book-entries.info?Id= and check its Folder.Id (D13). The verdict is workaround under D11: non-EUR operations (no currency field) and non-numeric operation numbers cannot be posted, line_number is not stored, and extra per-request calls are needed. Other risks: Horus refuses unbalanced ODs and has no AllowUnbalanced option on this endpoint, Chift's default 'posted' sets Confirmed=true which removes the API delete option, updates create a new book-entry Id, and there is no idempotency key, so a timeout must be followed by a duplicate check and never by a blind retry.

Horus endpoints

  • PrimaryPOST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.new Creates the OD book entry (header + debit/credit lines). Returns only the new book entry Id. (1 per Chift call)
  • LookupPOST /daybooks.list Resolves the default journal when journal_id is null, with Filter.Types=[600] only (D6: 600 is the only miscellaneous_operation daybook type with a documented API posting endpoint, so the only one with blocked=false in Get journals). Use it only if exactly one daybook is returned, otherwise raise an error (Chift contract rule). Daybooks of Type 610/690/700 are exposed by Get journals as miscellaneous_operation with blocked=true (assumption) and are never auto-selected. The cached, folder-scoped list also serves journal_id validation. (cached reference data (1 per folder, refreshed periodically))
  • LookupGET /daybooks.info Validates a supplied journal_id: it exists, Data.Folder.Id == FolderId (D13 cross-folder guard, otherwise 400) and Type == 600 (D6; 610/690/700 are blocked journals and rejected). Alternative: the cached daybooks.list result, which is folder-scoped by construction. (0-1 per Chift call (or served from cache))
  • EnrichmentGET /folders.info Checks that the analytics module is enabled (WithAnalytics) before sending CostLines. Gives the legislation (BE/LU) as context. Has no base-currency field. (cached reference data (1 per folder))
  • OptionalPOST /folders.list Folder fallback of the shared folder rule (D7), used only when the Chift query folder_id is null and no folder selection is stored for the connection: use the folder if POST /folders.list {Mode: 0} returns exactly one, otherwise 400 'folder_id required'. Not called when exactly one folder was selected at activation. (0-1 per Chift call (cached))
  • AlternativePOST /cost-sections.info Not used by this route (D8). It takes only an Id (no FolderId) and returns no Folder, so it cannot prove that an analytic_account belongs to the target folder (D13). The folder-scoped analytics cache (cost-schemas.list + cost-sections.list) replaces it. (0 for this route (D8))
  • AlternativePOST /cost-schemas.info Not used by this route (D8). Schema SearchKey, Mandatory and CostSectionType come from the cached cost-schemas.list. cost-schemas.info does return Data.Folder.Id, but D8 forbids per-call .info lookups on write routes. (0 for this route (D8))
  • LookupPOST /cost-schemas.list First half of the single per-folder analytics cache (D8). Load all schemas once per folder and keep SearchKey (posted as SchemaSearchKey), Mandatory and CostSectionType. Only List-type schemas (100) feed Chift analytics; Free schemas (900) are excluded in v1 (D8). Also used to spot Mandatory schemas that a single Chift analytic_account cannot satisfy. (cached reference data (1 per folder, D8))
  • LookupPOST /cost-sections.list Second half of the per-folder analytics cache (D8): one call per List-type (100) schema with Filter.IncludeInactive=true, so that an inactive section can be told apart from an unknown one. Resolves the Chift analytic_account (cost-section GUID Id) locally to its SearchKey (posted as Value), its parent CostSchema.Id and Inactive. A section that is not in this folder's cache is rejected, which also enforces the cross-folder guard (D13). (cached reference data (1 per folder x List-type cost schema, D8))
  • LookupPOST /fiscal-years.list Called with Filter.Dates=[operation_date] and Filter.IncludeFuture=true, for two uses. (a) Required whenever operation_number is supplied (D12): the StartDate/EndDate of the fiscal year containing operation_date give the StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear period of the book-entries.list duplicate pre-check. (b) Optional closed-period pre-check: a fiscal year should contain operation_date, and the date should fall strictly after LastMiscOperationsClosureDate. The comparison rule is a deduction (derived), and the field is an untyped string|null, so it must be parsed defensively. The connector can then refuse or warn cleanly instead of relying on an undocumented Horus error. (0-1 per Chift call (required when operation_number is supplied; cacheable per folder))
  • OptionalGET /fiscal-years.info Optional: IsClosed flag, which only the .info variant returns. (0-1 per Chift call (cacheable))
  • OptionalPOST /accounts.list Optional pre-validation that every account_number exists as an account SearchKey. The filter is starts-with, so match exactly on the client side. Exposes Inactive and IsSummary flags (posting rule for them undocumented). Also resolves account Ids (e.g. a company's concentration account) to SearchKeys from the cache. (1 per Chift call with SearchKeys array (or cached chart of accounts))
  • OptionalPOST /companies.list Optional validation of partner_id: the company (third party) exists in the folder and is IsCustomer (Type 1) or IsSupplier (Type 2). The FolderId in the request keeps the check folder-scoped. Optionally compares the line's account_number with the company's CustomerConcentrationAccount / SupplierConcentrationAccount (an Accounts Id reference, resolved to a SearchKey through the cached accounts.list, or GET /accounts.info accepted only if its Data.Folder.Id = FolderId, D13). GET /companies.info?Id= is equivalent but Id-only, so its Data.Folder.Id must equal FolderId, otherwise 400 (D13). (1 per distinct partner_id (N+1 if not cached))
  • OptionalGET /users.me Optional, at connection setup: detect whether the authorising user is an accountant (IsAccountant) or an entrepreneur, to anticipate permission errors (403) when posting or confirming entries. (once per connection (cached))
  • Follow-upGET /book-entries.info Primary read-back after creation. Takes only the Id returned by .new, with no FolderId, DaybookId or period. Returns DocumentNo (the generated number when AutoGenerateNumber was sent), Confirmed, Date, Daybook.Id, Folder.Id and the AccountHistories line references (Type/Id only, no accounts or amounts). Cross-folder guard (D13): the read-back is accepted only if Data.Folder.Id == FolderId (and Data.Daybook.Id == the DaybookId used). Used when AutoGenerateNumber was sent, or when the response must reflect what Horus stored at entry level. It is not in the guide's list of sideloading endpoints, so line content needs account-histories.list or account-histories.info. It cannot serve the duplicate check after a lost POST response, because the Id is then unknown. (0-1 per Chift call (only if the number is auto-generated or the stored state must be confirmed))
  • Follow-upPOST /account-histories.list Line-level read-back, only when full line fidelity is wanted (GET /book-entries.info returns line references only). One call with Filter.BookEntryId = new Id (no DaybookId needed) returns the stored lines: OrderNo, AccountSearchKey, Debit/Credit, Comment, AccountType, CompanyDetail, CostDetail, plus DocumentNo and Confirmed. The month/year period is mandatory: use the month of operation_date, and if nothing comes back take the exact AccountingMonth/AccountingYear from GET /account-histories.info. With Filter.DaybookIds + ModifiedAfter it also serves the heuristic duplicate check after an uncertain POST. (0-1 per Chift call (only if full line fidelity is wanted, or retry after timeout))
  • AlternativeGET /account-histories.info Resolves one AccountHistories reference returned by GET /book-entries.info, with no FolderId or period: OrderNo, AccountSearchKey, Debit/Credit, Comment, AccountType, CompanyDetail, CostDetail and AccountingMonth/AccountingYear. Two uses: (a) one call on the first reference to get the exact accounting period when account-histories.list finds nothing for the month of operation_date; (b) per-line read-back instead of account-histories.list (N calls, so only to fetch missing lines or for very small entries). It returns no Folder field, so the D13 guard is applied transitively: call it only on references taken from a book-entries.info response whose Folder.Id was verified, and check Data.BookEntry.Id == the created Id. (0-N per Chift call (1 per line reference; normally 0 or 1))
  • LookupPOST /book-entries.list Duplicate pre-check before every create that carries an operation_number, drafts included (D12): {FolderId, DaybookId, StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear = the fiscal year containing operation_date (StartDate/EndDate from fiscal-years.list), Filter:{DocumentNo: Number}}. Compare DocumentNo exactly on the client side; a match means the number is already used and the create is refused (the daybook + fiscal-year uniqueness scope is an assumption, A). Also run after a timeout or 500, before any retry: Filter.DocumentNo when the number is known, or Filter.ModifiedAfter (heuristic) when it was auto-generated. Not needed for read-back: GET /book-entries.info reads the entry by Id without DaybookId or period. Sideloading the AccountHistories array is not documented (assumption). (1 per Chift call when operation_number is supplied (D12), plus 0-1 after a timeout/500)
  • OptionalDELETE /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.delete Not part of the nominal flow. Possible compensation (for example, a draft created by mistake). Works only on unconfirmed entries created through the API. (0 (compensation only))
  • AlternativePOST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.update Not used by this create route. Relevant for any future Chift update: full replacement that returns a NEW book entry Id and deletes the original attachment. Its Lines schema has no VehicleId, unlike .new. (0 for this route)
  • AlternativePOST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.lines.update Not used by this create route. Partial line edit (Type, Account, Comment, MatchNo, CompanyId) by AccountHistoryId; amounts cannot change. (0 for this route)

Call sequence

  1. Map consumer_id to the Horus licence connection: licence-specific api_url plus Bearer token, refreshed once on 401. Resolve FolderId with the shared folder rule (D7): use query folder_id when given; if it is null and exactly one folder was selected at activation, use that folder; if no selection is stored, use the single folder returned by POST /folders.list {Mode: 0} (cached); otherwise return 400 'folder_id required'. Any Horus 403 is returned as Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' (D7).
  2. [Connector validation, no Horus call] Reject the request before calling Horus when any of these holds: currency != 'EUR', or currency_exchange_rate is neither null nor 1; lines is empty; a customer_account/supplier_account line has no partner_id; operation_number is present but not purely numeric, or does not round-trip after conversion (leading zeros, more than 15 digits); the lines do not balance (round each amount to 2 decimals and require sum(amount) == 0).
  3. [Journal] If journal_id is given: validate it against the cached daybooks.list, or GET /daybooks.info?Id=journal_id, and require Data.Folder.Id == FolderId (D13, otherwise 400) and Type == 600 (MiscOperation, D6). Daybooks of Type 610/690/700 are listed by Get journals as miscellaneous_operation with blocked=true (assumption), so they are rejected with 400 too. If journal_id is null: POST /daybooks.list {FolderId, Filter:{Types:[600]}}. Exactly one result → that is the DaybookId; otherwise return an error. Chift rule: 'automatically set if only one journal exists otherwise an error will be thrown'. This matches Get journals, where Type 600 is the only non-blocked miscellaneous_operation type (D6).
  4. [Analytics, only if a line has analytic_account] GET /folders.info (cached) → WithAnalytics must be true. Resolve each analytic_account (a Horus cost-section GUID Id, D8) only from the per-folder analytics cache: POST /cost-schemas.list once per folder, plus POST /cost-sections.list per List-type (CostSectionType 100) schema with Filter.IncludeInactive=true. Make no per-call cost-sections.info / cost-schemas.info lookups (D8). An id that is not in the cache (unknown, a section of another folder, or anything outside a List-type schema) → 400; a section with Inactive=true → 422 (D8). Keep the section SearchKey (Value), the parent schema SearchKey (SchemaSearchKey) and the folder's Mandatory schemas.
  5. [Fiscal year and optional pre-checks] - Fiscal year: POST /fiscal-years.list {FolderId, Filter:{Dates:[operation_date as ISO 8601], IncludeFuture:true}}. Required when operation_number is supplied, because step 5 needs the fiscal-year period (StartDate/EndDate → StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear) (D12); otherwise optional. Refuse or warn only if no fiscal year comes back even with IncludeFuture=true; Horus's own behaviour in that case is undocumented (to confirm). Require operation_date strictly after LastMiscOperationsClosureDate (derived); that field is an untyped string|null, so parse it defensively. GET /fiscal-years.info returns IsClosed. - Accounts: POST /accounts.list {FolderId, Filter:{SearchKeys:[distinct account_numbers]}}. The filter is starts-with, so require an exact SearchKey match. Flag Inactive and IsSummary accounts; whether Horus refuses them is to confirm. - Partners: for each distinct partner_id, POST /companies.list {FolderId, Filter:{Id}} (folder-scoped by FolderId). If GET /companies.info is used instead, require Data.Folder.Id == FolderId, otherwise 400 (D13). Check that the company exists and is IsCustomer (Type 1) or IsSupplier (Type 2). Optionally compare account_number with the company's Customer/SupplierConcentrationAccount, resolved to a SearchKey (to confirm).
  6. [Duplicate pre-check, D12: whenever operation_number is supplied, drafts included] POST /book-entries.list {FolderId, DaybookId, StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear = fiscal year from step 4, Filter:{DocumentNo: Number}}. Compare DocumentNo exactly on the client side. A match → reject with 400 'operation_number already exists in this journal and fiscal year' (with the existing book entry Id in the message) and do not create. Treating daybook + fiscal year as the uniqueness scope of Number is an assumption (assumption): Horus does not document Number uniqueness.
  7. [Build payload] Sort lines by line_number (stable sort). For each line: - Type from type. - Account = account_number. - amount > 0 → Credit = amount, Debit = 0; amount < 0 → Debit = -amount, Credit = 0. - Comment = description. - CompanyId = partner_id, only for Type 1/2. - If analytic_account: CostLines = [{Amount: |amount|, Costs: [{SchemaSearchKey, Value: section SearchKey}]}], both keys from the D8 cache. A single cost line carries 100% of the line, so the D8 largest-remainder rounding rule is trivially exact. Header: FolderId, DaybookId, Date = operation_date + 'T00:00:00+00:00'. Status and number cases (D12): - draft → Confirmed=false, Number = operation_number or 0. - posted with the per-consumer setting 'always push unconfirmed' on → Confirmed=false, Number = operation_number or 0. AutoGenerateNumber is not sent (it "Only works if "Confirmed" is set to *true*"), and the response status is 'draft'. - posted with a number → Confirmed=true, Number = operation_number. - posted without a number → Confirmed=true, AutoGenerateNumber=true, Number=0.
  8. Record timestamp T0 just before the call, with a safety margin for clock skew. Then POST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.new (Content-Type: application/json) → {Id}.
  9. [Read-back, conditional: AutoGenerateNumber was used, or the response must reflect what Horus stored] GET /book-entries.info?Id=Id: one call, no FolderId, DaybookId or period. Accept it only if Data.Folder.Id == FolderId and Data.Daybook.Id == DaybookId (D13); otherwise return 400 (not expected for an Id this call has just created in that folder). Take DocumentNo, Confirmed, Date, Daybook.Id and the AccountHistories[] references. Only if full line fidelity is wanted (book-entries.info returns line references only and is not a sideloading endpoint): POST /account-histories.list {FolderId, StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear = month of operation_date, Filter:{BookEntryId: Id, IncludeReport:false, IncludeCompanyDetail:true, IncludeCostDetail:true}} → OrderNo, AccountSearchKey, Debit, Credit, Comment, AccountType, CompanyDetail.Company.Id and CostDetail. Compare the returned Ids with the AccountHistories references (derived; whether that array includes IsReport lines is to confirm). If lines are missing: GET /account-histories.info?Id=<one reference> → AccountingMonth/AccountingYear, then repeat account-histories.list with that exact period, or resolve each missing reference with account-histories.info (1 call per line). account-histories.info has no Folder field: call it only on references from the verified book-entries.info and check Data.BookEntry.Id == Id (D13).
  10. Build MiscellaneousOperationOut: id = Id; journal_id = the DaybookId used (equal to Daybook.Id when read back); status from the Confirmed value actually sent (so 'draft' when 'always push unconfirmed' forced it, D12), or from book-entries.info when read back; operation_number = Number, or DocumentNo from book-entries.info, as a string (null if 0); operation_date = request date (or book-entries.info Date as YYYY-MM-DD); currency = 'EUR'; currency_exchange_rate = 1; lines echoed from the request, or rebuilt from the line read-back. Error mapping (map on Horus ErrorType, the machine-readable category, not on Message): - 400 → Chift 400/422 with Horus Details; do not retry. - 401 → refresh the token once and retry; if it fails again, ask the user to reconnect. - 403 → Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' (D7), with the Horus Details appended, because on this endpoint a 403 may also come from user rights (e.g. confirming entries). The Chift contract declares only 400/422/404. No retry. - 404 → Chift 404. - 500 or timeout → never blind-retry (D12; Horus has no idempotency key). First run a duplicate check (GET /book-entries.info cannot help, because the Id is lost with the response): book-entries.list Filter.DocumentNo when the number is known; otherwise book-entries.list (DaybookId + period) or account-histories.list (DaybookIds) with Filter.ModifiedAfter=T0, comparing date, accounts and amounts. Retry with exponential backoff only if no match is found.

Considerations and limitations

  • There is no currency or exchange-rate field on the misc-operation endpoints. Foreign currency exists only on the invoice endpoints, and folders.info exposes no base currency. Impact: The route is EUR-only. Chift operations in any other currency cannot be posted and must be rejected. currency_exchange_rate is accepted only as 1 or null. Derived
  • Strict balance: the OD must balance, and the miscellaneous operation schema has no AllowUnbalanced option (that option exists only on the invoice endpoints). Impact: The connector must validate sum(amount)==0, with rounding, before calling. Otherwise Horus returns 400. Documented
  • Confirmed (posted) entries cannot be deleted through the API. DELETE works only for non-confirmed entries injected through the API. Impact: Chift's default status 'posted' maps to Confirmed=true (D12) and makes the operation irreversible through the connector, unless the per-consumer 'always push unconfirmed' setting is on. Corrections need the update endpoint (which returns a new Id) or the accountant. Documented
  • Updates generate a new book entry Id (book-entries.*.update). Impact: The id returned by this route can become invalid after a later update. Chift must reconcile on daybook + number, not on Id alone. Documented
  • Horus Number is numeric. Auto-generation works only together with Confirmed=true. Impact: Alphanumeric Chift operation_number values are refused, and numeric strings lose leading zeros. A draft without a number is stored with Number 0, so no operation_number can be returned. Documented
  • Create returns only {Id}. GET /book-entries.info reads the entry back by that Id alone, but returns the lines only as Type/Id references and is not in the guide's list of sideloading endpoints. Impact: Header read-back (DocumentNo, Confirmed, Date, Daybook.Id) is one extra call with no period or daybook, needed when the number is auto-generated. Full line fidelity costs one more call: account-histories.list (Filter.BookEntryId, mandatory month/year period) or one account-histories.info per line. Documented
  • line_number has no writable Horus field. Impact: Line order is conveyed only through the order of the array (assumed to become OrderNo). Assumption
  • The single Chift analytic_account ('no distribution possible') maps to one cost section in one List-type cost schema. Horus supports up to 8 schemas, distribution keys and Free (type 900) schemas; Free schemas are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 (D8). Impact: Folders with several Mandatory cost schemas, or with a Mandatory Free schema, cannot be fully served from the Chift input. Whether Horus rejects such lines is undocumented. Derived
  • Partner information is meaningful only on Type 1/2 lines; CompanyId is 'Not used' for Type 3. Impact: A partner_id sent on a general_account line is dropped. Documented
  • Only daybook Type 600 is documented as the target of this endpoint (D6). Impact: The connector auto-selects and accepts only Type 600 journals. Get journals lists 610 TemporaryMiscOperation, 690 SystemMiscOperation and 700 CostMiscOperation as miscellaneous_operation but with blocked=true (assumption), so a journal_id pointing to them is rejected with 400 until Horus confirms they can be posted to. Documented
  • No idempotency key in Horus write endpoints. Impact: A retry after a timeout can create a duplicate, so the connector never blind-retries (D12). Whenever operation_number is supplied, a book-entries.list Filter.DocumentNo pre-check runs before the create (D12; the daybook + fiscal-year uniqueness scope is an assumption), and the same exact check runs after a timeout. With auto-numbering, detection after a timeout is only heuristic: query entries in the daybook/period with ModifiedAfter = pre-POST timestamp and compare date, accounts and amounts. Concurrent postings by other users can blur the result. Derived
  • Status values 'matched' and 'cancelled' cannot result from this create call. Impact: The create response is always 'draft' or 'posted'. Derived
  • MiscOperation lines have no beneficiary line type (only 1 customer, 2 supplier, 3 general), although Horus beneficiaries (e.g. employees) are Companies and wages daybooks (IsWages) exist. Impact: Low impact for Chift, which has no employee line type either. Payroll ODs needing per-employee sub-ledger detail cannot be represented through this route. Whether a beneficiary Company may be referenced by CompanyId on a Type 1/2 line is undocumented. Derived
  • Every create that carries an operation_number needs two extra calls before posting: fiscal-years.list for the fiscal-year period, and a book-entries.list Filter.DocumentNo duplicate pre-check (D12). Impact: Higher latency and API usage on numbered operations; this is part of why the route is rated workaround (D11). The pre-check assumes Number is unique per daybook and fiscal year, which Horus does not document. Derived

Send CostLines=[{Amount: abs(amount), Costs:[{SchemaSearchKey, Value: section SearchKey}]}]: one cost line carries 100% of the line (Chift allows no distribution), so the D8 largest-remainder rounding is trivially exact. The folder must be WithAnalytics. Leave CostDistributionKey null. Free schemas (CostSectionType 900) are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 (D8): they are not in the cache, so they cannot be addressed.DerivedChift analytic_account: "This is the id of a analytic account - no distribution possible."; CostLines[].Amount *: "The sum of all cost lines' amounts must be equal to the corresponding line's debit/credit amount."; SchemaSearchKey *: "The search key of an existing cost schema."; Value *: "The search key of an existing cost section, linked to this cost schema"; POST /cost-sections.list → Filter.IncludeInactive: "*FALSE* by default", Data[].Inactive; POST /cost-schemas.list → CostSectionType "100: List - expected values are cost sections - 900: Free"

Detailed open questions (15)
  • Horus Can miscellaneous operations be posted in a foreign currency at all, and is the folder base currency always EUR (BE/LU legislations)? The miscellaneous operation schema has no CurrencyCode/CurrencyRate and folders.info exposes no base currency. This decides whether Chift's required 'currency' field can be anything other than EUR.
  • Horus Is Number unique per daybook or fiscal year? Is a format enforced (e.g. YYYYMM + sequence, as in the example 201909001)? What happens on a duplicate? Needed to map Chift operation_number safely and to scope the D12 duplicate pre-check (book-entries.list Filter.DocumentNo over the daybook and fiscal year), which currently assumes daybook + fiscal-year uniqueness.
  • Horus Can every integration or licence set Confirmed=true, or can the fiduciary restrict this? Can a connection authorised by an entrepreneur (IsAccountant=false) post and confirm miscellaneous operations, or will Horus return 403? Does confirmation also lock edits through .update and .lines.update? Chift's default status is 'posted', and confirmed entries cannot be deleted through the API. This determines whether status=posted is achievable for SMB-licence connections and how to map a 403.
  • Horus Which daybook types does book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.new accept: only 600, or also 610 TemporaryMiscOperation and wages daybooks (IsWages)? Determines which journals the connector exposes as valid targets and how journal auto-selection behaves. Until answered, D6 accepts only 600 and marks 610/690/700 as blocked (assumption).
  • Horus Are CostLines accepted on Type 1/2 lines and on balance-sheet accounts? What happens if the folder has several Mandatory cost schemas and only one is provided, or if a Mandatory schema is Free (CostSectionType 900)? What value format do Free schemas take in CostLines? Chift sends one analytic_account per line; we need to know when Horus will reject. Free schemas are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 (D8) until this is answered.
  • Horus Does Horus enforce that a Type 1/2 line uses the company's customer/supplier concentration account? Can posting target Inactive or summary (IsSummary) accounts? Chift sends account_number freely; these rules drive pre-validation and error mapping.
  • Horus What is the error (ErrorType) when posting into a closed period or outside any fiscal year, on an account requiring a vehicle without VehicleId, or with a zero-amount line? Is there a list of ErrorType values? Needed for pre-validation and for mapping errors to Chift 400/422.
  • Horus What is the format of LastMiscOperationsClosureDate, and is posting on that exact date allowed? Which fiscal years count as 'future' for IncludeFuture? Needed for an exact closed-period and fiscal-year pre-check.
  • Horus Is the order of the Lines array persisted as OrderNo? Is the AccountHistories array of GET /book-entries.info ordered by OrderNo, and does it include IsReport lines? Could sideloading of AccountHistories be enabled on book-entries.info? Line order must be preserved for Chift line_number. The reference list is used to check that a line read-back is complete, and sideloading would return the entry and its lines in one call without a period (Guide: Sideloading "If you need it on another endpoint, contact us").
  • Horus Does the book entry Id also change when the accountant edits an entry in Horus Office (not only through API updates)? Affects the stability of the id returned to Chift and of GET-by-id routes.
  • Chift D12 maps 'posted' to Confirmed=true by default, with a per-consumer 'always push unconfirmed' setting. Which default fits fiduciary clients on Horus: keep Confirmed=true, or turn 'always push unconfirmed' on by default for Horus connections? Product decision (D12): Horus's API philosophy is accountant review of unconfirmed entries, while Chift defaults to 'posted', and confirmed entries cannot be deleted through the API.
  • Chift Should non-EUR operations and non-numeric operation_number values be rejected, or handled another way (e.g. the number put in a comment)? Is normalising leading zeros acceptable? Chift requires 'currency', and operation_number is a free string. Horus supports neither case.
  • Chift Please confirm the D8 convention for this route: analytic_account is the Horus cost-section GUID Id exposed by Get analytic accounts, and Free (900) schemas are neither exposed nor accepted in v1. Is a 400 on an unknown id (and a 422 on an inactive section) acceptable, or should the connector drop the analytic link with a warning? D8 fixes the id convention across routes; the remaining choice is the error policy for analytic ids that cannot be resolved.
  • Chift D6 maps 600/610/690/700 to miscellaneous_operation, with 610/690/700 blocked=true, and this route auto-selects only Type 600. Should IsWages (Type 600) daybooks be excluded from auto-selection, given that a wages daybook next to a general OD daybook makes auto-selection fail? The 'only one journal' rule must match what Get journals exposes as non-blocked. Wages daybooks are Type 600 and therefore not blocked.
  • Chift Does Chift core already validate line balance and partner_id presence, or must the connector do it? Is the D7 mapping of every Horus 403 to 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' acceptable for this route, where a 403 may also come from user rights (confirming entries)? Avoids duplicated or missing validation and fixes the error codes.
Analytic plans retrieval and analytic accounts retrieval (multiple plans)Supported Supported with workaroundGET …/accounting/analytic-plans · GET …/accounting/analytic-accounts/multi-analytic-plansCost schemas are analytic plans (one call). Cost sections are analytic accounts, listed with one call per schema; balances are not stored and can only be computed over a period.

A Horus cost schema is an analytic plan and a cost section is an analytic account. Plans come from one call. Accounts need one call per list-type schema (at most eight), merged and paginated by the connector. Horus stores no balance per analytic account, so balance, debit and credit can only be computed over a period, which the Chift route does not carry.

Detailed analysis

Horus calls analytics "cost" analytics. A cost schema is an analytical dimension, which is the Chift analytic plan. A cost section is a value inside one schema, which is the Chift analytic account. Guide: Data model: "a cost schema is an analytical dimension (e.g. DEPARTMENT ). A folder can use up to 8 cost schemas;" and "a cost section is a value within a schema (e.g. OFFICE )".

Both routes read one per-folder analytics cache (D8): POST /cost-schemas.list {FolderId}, plus POST /cost-sections.list once per "100: List" schema with Filter.IncludeInactive=true (cost-sections.list requires both FolderId and CostSchemaId). That is at most 1+8 Horus calls per cache fill. The same cache is the only resolver the write routes (Invoice creation, Miscellaneous operation creation) use to turn Chift ids into Horus SchemaSearchKey / Value and to reject inactive sections with 422 (D8, D13). Ids are fixed by D8: analytic_plan = cost schema GUID, analytic_account = cost section GUID. "900: Free" schemas have no cost sections and are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 (D8). folder_id follows the shared D7 rule.

Get Analytic Plans is a 1:1 read of the cached schema list (supported, D11). Get analytic accounts (multi plans) merges the per-schema section lists and paginates inside Chift (workaround).

Cost sections carry no currency and no balance. balance/credit/debit can only be computed by summing POST /cost-histories.list rows per CostSection<OrderNo>. That endpoint needs a month/year period that the Chift route does not carry, it includes unconfirmed entries, it has no ConfirmedOnly filter, and its Balance sign convention is undocumented.

Both routes depend on the folder's Analytics module (WithAnalytics on folders.list/folders.info).

Main risks:

  • the policy for balances (0, null or a computed period)
  • loss of Free-type dimensions in v1 (D8) and of the schema/section hierarchy
  • null and {} values in responses
  • cache staleness: the cost endpoints have no ModifiedAfter filter, and the write routes depend on the same cache
  • the docs conflict on how far a SearchKey is unique (per folder in Guide: Data model, "linked to this cost schema" in the posting reference). GUID ids (D8) keep posting unambiguous, but code alone may be ambiguous

Horus endpoints

  • PrimaryPOST /cost-schemas.list Primary source for Get Analytic Plans (one schema = one Chift plan, id = schema GUID, D8) and the first half of the single per-folder analytics cache (D8) shared with the write routes. It gives the schema Ids, the SearchKeys (needed to post SchemaSearchKey), CostSectionType (only List schemas have sections) and OrderNo. Free (900) schemas stay in the cache flagged as Free so the write routes can reject them, but they are never returned to Chift (D8). (1 per folder cache fill or refresh (D8; at most 8 rows); 0 per Chift call when the cache is warm)
  • PrimaryPOST /cost-sections.list Primary source for the analytic accounts and the second half of the D8 per-folder cache. Called once per List-type schema, because CostSchemaId is mandatory, and always with Filter.IncludeInactive=true, so the cache holds inactive sections: reads return them with active=false and the write routes reject them with 422 (D8). The cache is the only resolver of section GUID → (SchemaSearchKey, Value) for posting (D8, D13). There is no per-call lookup by Filter.SearchKeys or cost-sections.info; a cache miss triggers a refresh of the whole folder cache. (1 per cost schema with CostSectionType=100 per cache fill (N+1, N <= 8))
  • LookupGET /folders.info Pre-check that the folder has the Analytics module (WithAnalytics) before calling the cost endpoints. If it does not, return an empty page. (1 per folder (cached reference data))
  • LookupPOST /folders.list Two uses. (1) D7 fallback: when no folder selection is stored for the connection, call POST /folders.list {Mode: 0} and use the single folder returned; if there is more than one, return Chift 400 'folder_id required'. (2) Alternative to folders.info for the WithAnalytics gate: the folder list (probably already called by the Chift folders route) returns WithAnalytics per folder and can filter on it. (1 per consumer (cached); only when the D7 fallback or the WithAnalytics gate needs it)
  • OptionalPOST /cost-histories.list Optional enrichment, and the only analytics-specific source of movements. Sum Debit/Credit/Balance per cost section to fill balance/credit/debit. The response includes the folder's schema list with OrderNo, so the CostSection<OrderNo> join can be done from the same response. Only references whose Id is in the D8 cache are aggregated; Free-schema values (excluded by D8) are ignored. Send Content-Encoding: gzip, because a full-year payload can be large and the endpoint has no paging. (1 per Chift call per period (returns all schemas at once; may be large))
  • OptionalPOST /daybooks.list Optional, balance path only. Resolves the Ids of Budget (900) daybooks, and of CostMiscOperation (700) daybooks if policy requires it, to pass as cost-histories.list Filter.ExcludedDaybookIds. cost-histories.list cannot filter by daybook type directly. (1 per folder (cached reference data))
  • OptionalPOST /fiscal-years.list Optional. Works out a default period (for example the current fiscal year) for cost-histories.list, because the Chift route has no date parameters. StartDate/EndDate (date-time) must be converted into StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear. (1 per folder (cached))
  • AlternativePOST /account-histories.list Alternative balance source. General-ledger lines with Filter.IncludeCostDetail=true give CostDetail.CostLines[].Debit/Credit per Costs[].CostSchema/CostSection. It is heavier than cost-histories.list, but it is the only documented way to compute confirmed-only analytic balances (Filter.ConfirmedOnly) and to sync incrementally (ModifiedAfter). It includes report (carry-forward) lines by default (IncludeReport TRUE), so the policy must decide whether IsReport lines count. Supports sideloading. (1 per period (large payloads))
  • AlternativePOST /account-balance.list Considered and rejected. This is the general-ledger trial balance per account search key, with no cost-schema or cost-section dimension, so it cannot feed analytic balances. Listed so the reader sees it was checked. (not called)
  • AlternativePOST /accounts.list Considered and rejected. Accounts with AccountCategory 1200 CostInc / 1300 CostExp are general-ledger accounts in the chart of accounts, not cost sections. They must not be mapped to Chift analytic accounts. (not called)
  • AlternativePOST /cost-schemas.info Not used. Under D8 and D13, analytics are resolved only from the folder-scoped cache, never by per-call .info lookups, and this also applies to the write routes. Listed so the reader knows it exists; the reference documents it as POST with a body Id. (not called (D8: cache only))
  • AlternativePOST /cost-sections.info Not used, not even for Get analytic account by id or for write-route resolution (D8, D13: folder-scoped cache only). derived: its response has no Folder reference (only CostSchema.Id), so an Id-only lookup could not be folder-guarded without a second call. The cache avoids that. (not called (D8: cache only))

Call sequence

  1. GET analytic-plans, step 1: resolve the Horus FolderId with the shared D7 rule. Use query folder_id (the Horus folder GUID exposed by the Chift folders route) when it is given. Otherwise: if exactly one folder was selected at activation, use it; if no selection is stored, use the single folder returned by POST /folders.list {Mode: 0}; in every other case return Chift 400 'folder_id required'. Every Horus cost endpoint requires FolderId.
  2. Step 2 (cached per folder): GET /folders.info?Id={FolderId} (or reuse the folders.list cache) and read Data.WithAnalytics. If false, return {items: [], total: 0, page, size} and make no further calls.
  3. read the D8 per-folder analytics cache. If it is cold or expired, fill it: POST /cost-schemas.list {"FolderId": FolderId} (at most 8 schemas), then the section lists of accounts step 4, so every analytic route (and the write routes) share one snapshot.
  4. drop Free-type schemas (CostSectionType 900). They are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 (D8). Log a warning for folders that have them, so support can explain the missing dimension.
  5. map each remaining schema to an AnalyticPlanItem: id=Id (schema GUID, D8), name=Heading, active=true, parent_id=MasterCostSchema.Id. Treat null, {} or a missing Id as no parent. Sort by OrderNo, then slice by page/size in Chift; total = count of List schemas.
  6. GET analytic-accounts/multi-analytic-plans, steps 1-3: same as above (D7 folder resolution, WithAnalytics gate, D8 cache with cost-schemas.list). Reuse the cached snapshot when possible.
  7. Step 4 (cache fill, D8): for each schema with CostSectionType = 100 (List), call POST /cost-sections.list {"FolderId", "CostSchemaId": schema.Id, "Filter": {"IncludeInactive": true}}. That is N+1 with N <= 8. Calls can run in parallel, but keep concurrency modest because no rate limits are documented. Store the result in the single per-folder cache (Guide: Reading accounting data: "Load it once and cache it per folder"); Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation read the same snapshot (D8).
  8. Step 5 (optional, only if balances are required; the policy must be agreed with Chift): (a) POST /fiscal-years.list {FolderId, Filter: {Dates: [today]}} and convert StartDate/EndDate (date-time) into StartMonth/StartYear/EndMonth/EndYear. (b) Optionally POST /daybooks.list {FolderId, Filter: {Types: [900]}} (cached) to collect the Budget daybook Ids. (c) POST /cost-histories.list {FolderId, StartMonth, StartYear, EndMonth, EndYear, Filter: {ExcludedDaybookIds: budget Ids}} with the header Content-Encoding: gzip.
  9. Step 6 (aggregation): map OrderNo to schema using Data.CostSchemas[] from the same cost-histories.list response, so the join cannot drift from a stale cached schema list. For each row and each N in 1..8 where CostSectionN is not empty (not null, not {}, and has an Id), add Debit and Credit (null = 0) and Balance to section CostSectionN.Id. A row counts once in every schema it touches; never sum across schemas. Ignore references whose Id is not in the D8 cache (for example Free-schema values, excluded by D8).
  10. build the AnalyticAccountItemOutMultiAnalyticPlans rows: id=Id (section GUID, D8), code=SearchKey, name=Heading, active=(Inactive !== true), analytic_plan=CostSchema.Id (schema GUID, D8), currency='EUR' when balances were computed in step 6 (D4 assumption) and null otherwise, balance/credit/debit from step 6 or the agreed default. Sort deterministically (schema OrderNo, then SearchKey), then slice by page/size; total = merged count (List schemas only).
  11. Error handling (every Horus call): 401: refresh the access token once and retry. 400: do not retry; log Details. 500: retry with exponential backoff (Guide: Responses & errors). A persistent Horus 403 becomes Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' (D7). A persistent 404 (unknown FolderId) becomes a Chift 400 ChiftError naming the folder. Chift 400 is the only non-validation error both Chift routes declare.
  12. Cache refresh (D8, D13): use a short TTL, plus one full refresh of the folder cache when a write route meets an unknown plan/account GUID; if it is still unknown after the refresh, reject. Never fall back to cost-schemas.info / cost-sections.info.

Considerations and limitations

  • Schemas with CostSectionType "900: Free" have no enumerable cost sections. Their values are typed as "100: Characters", "200: Digits" or "300: Date", optionally with a mask. cost-sections.new says the parent schema "must be of type *List* (CostSectionType = 100)". Impact: They are excluded from Chift analytics in v1 (D8): not returned by Get analytic plans/accounts, not accepted on write, and omitted on read with a warning. A folder that analyses by a Free dimension loses that dimension in Chift. Free values probably appear only in cost histories as CostSection<N>SearchKey strings (assumption). Derived
  • Cost sections have no balance, debit, credit or currency fields: the response schema lacks them. Impact: balance/credit/debit stay at the Chift default (0, which reads as a real zero) unless cost-histories.list is aggregated over a period chosen without input from the Chift route. currency is 'EUR' only when balances are computed (D4 assumption) and null otherwise. Derived
  • N+1 pattern: cost-sections.list requires CostSchemaId (documented: "CostSchemaId *"), so the connector makes one call per List schema. Impact: Bounded: at most 1 + 8 calls, plus a cached folders.info. Acceptable, and cacheable as reference data. Derived
  • No pagination parameters on any Horus list endpoint. Impact: Chift must fetch everything and slice. total is only known after the full fetch. Derived
  • Section hierarchy (MasterCostSection, child CostSections) and categories (Categorized/CategoryHeading, Category) have no field on AnalyticAccountItemOutMultiAnalyticPlans. Impact: Hierarchy and grouping are lost (flattened). It is unknown whether cost-sections.list returns child sections at top level. To confirm
  • Cost schemas expose no active/inactive flag. Impact: Plan active is always true; a disused schema cannot be signalled. Derived
  • Id convention, decided in D8: analytic_plan = cost schema GUID, analytic_account = cost section GUID. AnalyticPlanItem has no slot for the schema SearchKey (only id, name, active, parent_id), while posting needs SchemaSearchKey and the section Value (SearchKey). Impact: Every analytic write (invoice and misc-operation routes) translates GUIDs into SchemaSearchKey / Value through the single per-folder cache (D8). A lone account GUID on a misc-operation line still resolves to its schema, so the per-schema SearchKey ambiguity does not affect posting. The cost: the write routes depend on a fresh cache, and the plan SearchKey stays invisible to Chift users. Derived
  • cost-histories.list has no ConfirmedOnly filter (only CostSchemaIds, DaybookIds, ExcludedDaybookIds, IncludeCompanyDetail). Guide: Reading accounting data: "By default, lists include unconfirmed entries". Impact: Balances computed from cost histories include entries not yet validated by the accountant. Confirmed-only figures require the heavier account-histories.list path (Filter.ConfirmedOnly + IncludeCostDetail). Derived
  • The cost-histories.list response types CostHistories as an object, although the page text says the response "will also contain a list of all cost histories". Impact: The real response shape must be checked in a test folder before writing the aggregation. To confirm
  • Posting endpoints reference cost distribution keys (CostDistributionKey: "A cost distribution key as defined in Horus Office"), but none of the 72 reference endpoints lists them. Impact: They cannot be exposed as plans or accounts. This also affects the multi-plan invoice routes. Derived
  • Cost endpoints work only for folders with the Analytics module (WithAnalytics). The docs do not say what cost-schemas.list returns for a folder without it. Impact: The connector must gate on WithAnalytics and return an empty page (or a Chift error). Otherwise it risks an undocumented Horus 400/403. To confirm
  • Sideloading is not available on cost endpoints. Guide: Sideloading lists only book-entries.list, account-histories.list, companies.list and invoices.sales.outstanding.list. cost-histories.list has no paging. Impact: A full-year cost-histories.list response can be large, so send Content-Encoding: gzip (Guide: Making requests: "Gzip compression is available on every endpoint and highly recommended"). Sideloading would not have removed the schema→sections N+1 anyway, because schemas do not reference their sections. Derived
  • Schema Mandatory and section IsLocked/IsDefault have no Chift field, and their meaning is undocumented. Impact: Chift users cannot see that a dimension is mandatory or that a section may be locked. Posting could fail if a mandatory schema gets no value or a locked section is used. This is unverified. To confirm
  • Cache freshness (D8): cost-schemas.list and cost-sections.list have no ModifiedAfter filter, and no webhook documentation was provided. Impact: A section created, renamed or deactivated in Horus Office is invisible to Chift until the cache refreshes (TTL or refresh on a miss). Between refreshes, a write route may accept a section the accountant has just deactivated; how Horus then reacts is undocumented. Derived
