Call sequence

  1. Resolve consumer_id to the stored Horus connection record: license api_url, access token, single-use refresh token and expiry. Refresh proactively before expiry. FolderId follows the shared D7 rule: folder_id when given. If folder_id is null, use the single folder selected at activation; if no selection is stored, use the single folder returned by POST /folders.list {Mode: 0}; otherwise return Chift 400 'folder_id required'. Send 'Content-Encoding: gzip' on every Horus call below (Guide: Making requests).
  2. [both types, cached] POST /daybooks.list {FolderId} -> maps Id -> {SearchKey, Type} and SearchKey -> Id, and Type -> journal_type through the shared D6 table. On a miss, refresh once (daybooks.update can change SearchKey).
  3. [type=client] POST /invoices.sales.outstanding.list {FolderId, Filter:{ConfirmedOnly: (unposted_allowed == 'false')}}, with CompanyIds omitted (not []). Option (assumption, pending Horus confirmation): add "Include": "AccountHistory,Daybook" and read AccountSearchKey, MatchDetail and CurrencyRate from Included.AccountHistories, which would make step 3a unnecessary.
  4. [type=client] POST /customers.outstanding {FolderId} (Filter omitted, or no CompanyIds) -> index Data.AccountHistories[] by Id (AccountSearchKey, MatchDetail.MatchNo, CurrencyCode, CurrencyRate, IsReport, DocumentNo, Comment, Confirmed, DaybookSearchKey). Run in parallel with 2a.
  5. [type=client] Clean and join. (i) Drop top-level rows whose AccountHistory.Id appears in another row's LinkedElements[] (already netted into that invoice's OpenAmount). (ii) Drop rows with OpenAmount == 0 and log them (to confirm). (iii) Join 2a.Data[].AccountHistory.Id = 3a.AccountHistories[].Id. Rows missing from 3a -> GET /account-histories.info?Id=... (rare N+1). account-histories.info has no Folder field, so the D13 guard runs through the book entry: accept the line only if its Data.BookEntry.Id equals the row's BookEntry.Id, or else GET /book-entries.info on that BookEntry.Id shows Data.Folder.Id = FolderId. A proven mismatch -> Chift 400; an unverifiable line (BookEntry null) -> skip the item and log. (iv) Reconcile (assumption): emit customers.outstanding lines that no sales-list row references (top-level or LinkedElements) and log them. They use the customer-side ledger rule: amount = Debit − Credit, open_amount = the line's amount or match-group residual Σ(Debit − Credit) (D5), in EUR with rate 1 (D4). Keep AccountType 100, and apply Confirmed==true when unposted_allowed=='false'. (v) Control check (derived): compare Σ OpenAmount of the kept sales-list rows (EUR view) plus the reconciliation residuals with SumDebit − SumCredit of 3a (customer-side sign, D5), and log any gap.
  6. [type=supplier] POST /suppliers.outstanding {FolderId} (Filter omitted, or no CompanyIds) -> Data.AccountHistories[].
  7. [type=supplier] Post-filters: keep Confirmed == true when unposted_allowed == 'false'; keep AccountType 200 (defensive, since the docs example is a placeholder); drop MatchDetail.MatchStatus 900 'Totally' (defensive: the guide says outstanding items are lines not matched yet). amount = Credit − Debit (supplier side, D5). A line with MatchNo null gets open_amount = amount. If lines with a non-null MatchNo come back ('0: Partially', A): group by (CompanyDetail.Company.Id, MatchNo), compute residual = Σ(Credit − Debit) (D5), allocate it to the positive (invoice-side) lines oldest DueDate first, and drop fully consumed payment lines.
  8. [type=supplier] journal_id from the daybooks cache (DaybookSearchKey); journal_type from its Type via the shared D6 table. Amounts are returned in EUR with currency 'EUR' and currency_exchange_rate 1, whatever CurrencyCode says (D4, A); a foreign CurrencyCode is logged. Optional payment_communication for lines in a '100: Purchase' or '150: PurchaseCreditNote' daybook: CompanyDetail.RemarkPayment of the line's book entry (assumption), read in step 4c (GET /book-entries.info per distinct BookEntry.Id by default; POST /book-entries.list per daybook/period as the bulk alternative).
  9. [both, optional enrichment through BookEntry.Id] Collect the distinct BookEntry.Id values of: (i) supplier lines in a purchase daybook (RemarkPayment -> payment_communication, A); (ii) client reconciliation lines from 4a(iv), which have no PaymentRemark (same); (iii) IsReport=true lines on either side (EntryType, Daybook.Id -> original_document, A); (iv) only if the fallback is enabled, invoice-type lines with DueDate null (CompanyDetail.DueDate, A); (v) lines on either side whose partner-line Comment is null or empty (CompanyDetail.RemarkCompany -> reference, second step of the D3 chain, A). Skip Ids already in a cross-request cache keyed by BookEntry.Id (derived: an update generates a new book entry, so an invoice update cannot change what a cached Id returned). Call GET /book-entries.info?Id=... for the remaining Ids, with bounded concurrency and the token/500 handling of step 6. A 400/404 (entry replaced since the snapshot) leaves the enrichment null without failing the request. Cross-folder guard (D13): every response must have Data.Folder.Id = FolderId, otherwise return Chift 400. Use an entry's RemarkPayment, RemarkCompany or DueDate only if its CompanyDetail.Company.Id equals the line's partner_id. Cost rule (derived): .info costs 1 small call per distinct entry and nothing on a warm cache. POST /book-entries.list {FolderId, DaybookId, Start/End = min/max AccountingMonth/AccountingYear of the group} costs 1 call per daybook but returns every entry of that window, matched or not. Use .list for a daybook group only on a cold cache with many distinct entries and a short window; tune the threshold on a test license, since rate limits are undocumented (assumption).
  10. [both] Map to OutstandingItem. journal_type comes from the daybook Type via the shared D6 table. amount/open_amount use the side-based sign rule (D5). Each item has one currency view: EUR with rate 1 wherever invoice-currency amounts are missing (D4). reference = the first non-empty value of partner-line Comment -> RemarkCompany -> Remark, split on the first ' | ' for invoice-daybook lines (D3). Sort deterministically (date asc, then id asc), set total = item count, return slice [(page-1)*size, page*size). Cache the full result for a short TTL keyed by (consumer, folder, type, unposted_allowed).
  11. [errors, Guide: Responses & errors] Horus 401 -> refresh the token once and retry; if it fails again, return a Chift auth error asking the user to reconnect. Horus 400 -> no retry; log Details and return Chift 400 (ChiftError) carrying Horus Details (e.g. unknown FolderId). Horus 403 -> Chift 400 'folder not accessible with this connection' (D7). Horus 404 -> Chift 400 with an explicit error_code (not found), because the Chift contract declares only 400 and 422. A cross-folder mismatch found by the D13 guard -> Chift 400. Horus 500 -> retry with exponential backoff, then a Chift error. An invalid type or unposted_allowed is rejected with Chift 422 before any Horus call.
  12. ALT (only if Horus says folder-level outstanding calls are capped or incomplete). Step 1: POST /fiscal-years.list {IncludeFuture: true}, plus GET /fiscal-years.info per year to read IsClosed (each response checked for Data.Folder.Id = FolderId, D13). Step 2: POST /accounts.list to resolve the concentration accounts (from companies.list) to search keys. Step 3: POST /account-histories.list {FolderId, Start/End, Filter:{NotMatchedOnly: true, ConfirmedOnly: (unposted_allowed=='false'), IncludeCompanyDetail: true, IncludeMatchDetail: true, AccountSearchKeys: [concentration account keys]}}. Keep AccountType 100 or 200, apply the side-based sign rule (D5: Debit − Credit for 100, Credit − Debit for 200) and return amounts in EUR (D4). To avoid double counting, EITHER start at the first year that is not closed and keep IncludeReport at its default (true), OR read the full history with IncludeReport: false (assumption). Second option: POST /companies.list {IsCustomer|IsSupplier} and batch CompanyIds into customers/suppliers.outstanding.

6Dependencies and building blocks

Several requested routes depend on Chift routes outside the case list. Without them, a consumer cannot supply valid ids to the requested routes.

DependencyWhy the requested routes need itHorus supportBasis
VAT codes (GET vat-codes)Every invoice line requires tax_code, the id of a VAT code in the accounting software.No VAT-code entity or endpoint. Served from a connector-owned catalogue of valid Horus tax combinations, with one id grammar shared with invoice creation and retrieval.Derived
Supplier creation (POST suppliers)Purchase invoices reference the supplier by partner_id (optional in the specification; partner_info can create a missing supplier instead).Workaround, same as client creation: companies.new with IsSupplier = true and the same rules (search key, duplicate VAT check, read-back with companies.info).Derived
Clients and suppliers lists (GET clients / suppliers)Consumers look up existing partners before creating invoices.companies.list filtered on IsCustomer or IsSupplier; updated_after maps to ModifiedAfter. Chift’s free-text search (an OR over several fields) and pagination are emulated.Derived
Payment terms (GET payment-terms)Optional payment_term_id on invoices.No payment-term entity. Horus stores a compact delay code per company (for example 30E = 30 days end of month) and invoices take an explicit due date. Return a synthetic list or an empty list; payment_term_id is ignored on creation.Derived
Payment methods (GET payment-methods)Optional payment_method_id on invoices.Fixed PaymentMode list documented by Horus (bank transfer, domiciliation, card, PayPal, Doccle, Digiteal, check, bill draft…), returned as a constant list.Derived
Attachments (GET attachments)Invoices return attachments_info; files are fetched separately.book-entries.info gives the main document reference, then documents.download returns the bytes. Finding annexes needs a date-bounded documents.list.Derived
Bookyears (GET bookyears)Context for periods, closure checks and journal numbering.fiscal-years.list; the closed flag is documented only on fiscal-years.info (one call per year).Derived

Connector building blocks

These shared components serve several routes and drive most of the effort.

Building blockWhat it doesRoutes served
Connection and token managerStores api_url, access token, single-use refresh token and expiry per Horus license. Refreshes proactively, one refresh at a time per connection, and persists the new token pair before use.All
Folder resolutionOne rule for every route: folder_id is required when the connection has several selected folders; otherwise the single selected folder is used.All
Reference-data cache per folderDaybooks, chart of accounts, cost schemas and sections (including inactive ones), fiscal years and folder settings. Loaded once per folder, as the Horus guide recommends, and refreshed when a lookup misses (connector design).All write routes, invoices, journals, chart of accounts, outstanding items
Pagination and filter emulationHorus lists return everything. The connector fetches, filters by day, type or status, sorts deterministically and slices Chift pages, with a short-lived cache so consecutive pages do not refetch.All list routes
VAT-code catalogueConnector-owned list of valid Horus tax combinations with one id grammar, e.g. S-21-0-0 (sales: rate 21 %, tax origin 0, tax type 0) and P-21-2-0-0-1-true (purchases: rate, origin, type, kind, condition, condition value; see D2). Serves Chift VAT codes, decodes tax_code on write and encodes it on read.Invoice creation, invoice retrieval, VAT codes
Analytics converterTurns Chift per-plan percentages into Horus cost lines (amounts on combinations of cost sections) with largest-remainder rounding, and back.Invoice creation and retrieval, miscellaneous operations
Shared partner mapperOne mapping from a Horus company to a Chift client/supplier/partner, reused by every route that returns partner data.Client creation, invoices
Duplicate protectionSearch before create (VAT number, external reference, document number) and check before retrying after a timeout or server error, because Horus has no idempotency key.All write routes
Error translationMaps Horus status codes and ErrorType/Details to Chift errors; never forwards tokens.All

7Edge cases and error handling

Situations the connector must handle explicitly. Most follow from three Horus traits: no documented idempotency key, entries that get a new id when updated, and lists without documented pagination.

SituationProposed handlingRoutesBasis
A create call times out or returns 500 (client, invoice, operation, account).Never retry blindly. Search for the record first (external reference, VAT number, document number) and re-post only if it does not exist.All write routesDerived
The client’s VAT number already exists, as a customer or as a supplier only.Pre-check before creating. Return an “already exists” error with the existing id, and suggest force_merge when the match is a supplier.Client creationDerived
The invoice date falls in a period the accountant has closed.Check the fiscal-year closure dates before posting, as Horus recommends, and return a clear error. Horus offers no forced period on invoice creation.Invoice and operation creationDocumented
Header totals and line totals differ by rounding.Horus checks totals = base + tax and the sum of lines; tax within one cent is recalculated unless OverrideTaxAmounts is set. Validate in the connector first and use invoice_correction when needed.Invoice creationDocumented
An analytic split such as 33 / 33 / 34 %.Largest-remainder rounding so cost lines sum exactly to the line base amount.Invoice creationDerived
A tax_code that is not a valid Horus combination.Reject with 422 before calling Horus.Invoice creationDerived
A foreign-currency invoice or operation.Invoices: allowed when the folder accepts currencies (documented). Miscellaneous operations: the request has no currency field, so non-EUR operations are rejected (derived; proposed default, see C3). On read, ledger amounts are treated as EUR until Horus answers H4, and EUR amounts are never paired with a foreign currency code (assumption, D4).Invoices, operations, outstanding itemsDerived
The accountant edits an invoice created through the API.Horus generates a new book entry id, so the Chift id changes. Reconcile on daybook and document number; disclose this to consumers.Invoice retrieval and creationDocumented
A credit note stored in a sales or purchase daybook.Read both daybook types and classify each entry by the sign of the customer or supplier line; for open sales items, TransactionType overrides the sign.Invoice retrievalAssumption
Several possible journals and no journal_id.Auto-select by daybook type when exactly one candidate exists; otherwise 400 “journal_id required”.Invoice and operation creationDerived
An inactive account or cost section, or analytics sent to a folder without the analytics module.Reject with 422 before calling Horus.Write routesDerived
An id that belongs to another folder of the same license.Check the folder on every call by id; return 400.AllDerived
Partial payments and instalments.Open amounts come from matching groups. Horus has no instalment structure: an outstanding item has at most one due date, and an invoice has one due date per customer/supplier line (normally one for invoices posted through the API).Outstanding items, invoice retrievalDerived
An open item carried over from the previous fiscal year.Identify the opening entry through its entry type and link the original document where Horus provides it; otherwise leave original_document empty.Outstanding itemsAssumption
Two Chift calls for the same consumer refresh the token at the same time.Serialise refreshes per connection; refresh tokens are single-use.AllDocumented
A large folder (an accounting firm’s client with years of entries).Full lists with emulated pagination, narrow month windows, per-folder caching and gzip. Horus documents no size or rate limits.All list routesTo confirm

Error translation

Horus responseConnector behaviourChift response
401 UnauthorizedRefresh the token once (serialised per connection) and replay the call. If the refresh fails, mark the connection as disconnected.Re-authorisation needed when the refresh fails
400 Bad Request {ErrorType, Details, Message}Do not retry as-is. Use connector pre-checks to return specific errors (for example an existing client); otherwise pass Details through.400, or 422 for validation done by the connector
403 ForbiddenThe folder or resource is not accessible with this connection.400 “folder not accessible with this connection”
404 Not FoundUnknown id (for example a force_merge supplier or a replaced book entry).400 or 404, depending on the route
Duplicate found by the connector’s pre-check (VAT number, external reference or document number)Do not create.409 or 400 with the existing id, per question C6
500 / timeout on a readRetry with exponential backoff, as Horus advises.Error after retries
500 / timeout on a createNever retry blindly. Search for the record first (external reference, VAT number, document number), and re-post only if nothing is found.Created record, or an error

8Open questions

Consolidated and ordered by impact on the design. Each route card in section 4 also lists its detailed questions.

Questions for Horus

#QuestionWhy it mattersRoutes
H1Can you share the accepted tax-combination files for sales and purchases? Is an endpoint listing VAT codes or combinations available or planned? Can TaxKind be exposed on read?Invoice creation cannot post valid VAT without them, and Chift’s VAT codes depend on them.Invoices, VAT codes
H2Can a credit note (Type 1) be posted in a sales or purchase daybook, and an invoice in a credit-note daybook? Book entries do not expose the invoice type on read (TransactionType exists only on the open sales items, and document types only on documents.list): can it be exposed? Must credit-note amounts be positive?Decides daybook selection on write and invoice/refund classification on read.Invoices, journals
H3Is the document number unique per daybook and fiscal year? What happens on a duplicate number? Is there an idempotency key, or a way to get the next free number? Could invoices support automatic numbering?Duplicate protection and numbering for confirmed invoices.Invoices, operations, journals
H4In which currency are ledger amounts (debit/credit, book-entry totals) and invoice totals expressed for foreign-currency documents, and in which direction is CurrencyRate given? Is the folder base currency always EUR?Correct amounts on read for any foreign-currency document.Invoices, outstanding items, folders
H5Are there rate limits, maximum result sizes or maximum period spans on list endpoints?Lists are not paginated, and several routes make one call per daybook or per schema.All list routes
H6Which ErrorType values can be returned (duplicate VAT, search key or number, closed period, invalid tax combination, unknown account or cost section)? Do calls by id for an object in another folder return 403, 404 or 400?Specific error messages for Chift consumers and safe retry rules.All
H7What are the rules for search keys (length, characters, case) for companies and accounts? Is a cost section search key unique per folder or per schema?The connector must generate company search keys and validate account numbers.Clients, accounts, analytics
H8When the CompanyIds filter is omitted, do customers.outstanding and suppliers.outstanding return the open items of all partners, complete and without a cap? How are lines carried over from a previous year linked to their original document? Is a purchase-side equivalent of invoices.sales.outstanding.list planned?Determines the call pattern for outstanding items.Outstanding items
H9Where does the invoice header comment appear on read (partner-line comment, RemarkCompany or Remark)?Round-trip of reference and customer memo.Invoices, outstanding items
H10Do ledger period filters use calendar months or accounting periods? Does ModifiedAfter on book entries track changes to their lines and matching? Is there any signal for deleted or replaced entries (for example webhooks)?Reliable incremental synchronisation and date filtering.Invoices, outstanding items
H11What do CustomerActivitySuspended, Name2, Address2, Family and ContactPerson mean? How is an enterprise number written? Which concentration account applies when none is given?Mapping of active status, addresses, company numbers and receivable accounts.Clients
H12Can an account’s category be set or is it derived from the number? What is the full list of IntCategory values? Does accounts.list include inactive and title accounts?Chift account type on creation and in the chart of accounts.Accounts
H13What do the cost endpoints return for a folder without the analytics module? Must a cost line carry one value per cost schema? What do Mandatory, IsLocked, IsDefault and MasterCostSchema mean? Is the cost-history balance signed?Analytics on write and the analytic routes on read.Analytics, invoices, operations
H14What does IsUsed mean on daybooks? Can cash, temporary, system or cost daybooks receive entries through the API? Does fiscal-years.list return IsClosed?Journal flags and posting rules.Journals, operations
H15Which relationship paths can be sideloaded with Include on companies.list, book-entries.list and account-histories.list, and with which fields?Can cut read-backs and invoice retrieval to fewer calls.Clients, invoices
H16book-entries.info takes only an id: can it return an entry from another folder of the same license? What does it return for an entry that was replaced or deleted?Read-backs and single-invoice reads.Invoices, operations, outstanding items
H17Can Chift get a test license or test folder? Can test data be removed (there is no delete for companies or invoices)?The API works on live data.All write routes
H18What is the maximum attachment size for multipart uploads?Validation of PDFs sent with invoices.Invoices, operations

Questions for Chift

#QuestionWhy it mattersRoutes
C1For status “posted”, should the connector confirm entries in Horus, or push them unconfirmed by default so the accountant reviews them?Horus’s default for API entries is accountant review.Invoices, operations
C2Is a connector-owned VAT-code catalogue acceptable for GET vat-codes, and is returning tax_code = null on read when the code is ambiguous acceptable?Horus has no VAT-code entity.Invoices, VAT codes
C3For inputs Horus cannot store (withholding tax, Dutch G/N split, accrual dates, forced period, line quantity, extra addresses, non-EUR operations): reject or ignore with a warning? Can the connector return warnings?Consistent behaviour across Chift connectors.Clients, invoices, operations
C4For values Horus does not hold on read: null or a default (analytic balance 0 or null, approval status “unknown”, empty next document numbers)?Avoid misleading defaults.Invoices, journals, analytics
C5Which sign convention does Chift expect for refund amounts on input and for outstanding amounts on output?The case does not specify it.Invoices, outstanding items
C6On a duplicate (VAT number, external reference, document number): return an error (400/409) or the existing record? Which supplier fields may force_merge overwrite?Duplicate policy must match other connectors.Clients, invoices
C7Is rejecting non-numeric invoice and operation numbers acceptable?Horus numbers are numeric.Invoices, operations
C8What does partner_info.account_number mean: a third-party code or a ledger account?The Chift specification does not describe it.Invoice creation
C9Is deriving analytic combinations from independent per-plan percentages acceptable? Can “free” cost schemas stay out of scope in v1?Horus allocates amounts to combinations of cost sections.Invoices, analytics
C10Should unconfirmed Horus entries be returned as drafts or excluded by default? Is it acceptable that an invoice id changes when the accountant updates it?Read behaviour for entries under accountant review.Invoice retrieval
C11Which default period should invoice retrieval use when no dates are given?Horus requires a month window and has no pagination.Invoice retrieval
C12Should the Horus connector always enable the multiple-folders feature?Accounting-firm licenses hold many folders.All
C13Documentation details: FolderItem.main_currency is described as ISO 3166-1 (a country standard; ISO 4217 is meant), and the line tax_amount formula omits the division by 100.Small corrections to the Chift API reference.Folders, invoices

9Conclusion and next steps

A Horus connector is feasible for the full scope of the case. The Horus API covers every object Chift needs, with dedicated endpoints for the two most valuable flows (pushing sales and purchase invoices with their PDF, and reading open items). The work is in the translation layer: rebuilding invoices from the ledger, a VAT-code catalogue, analytics conversion, pagination emulation and duplicate protection. None of these needs changes on the Horus side, but a handful of answers from Horus would remove the main assumptions.

Proposed next steps

  1. Re-check the Chift specification used here against the live documentation (docs.chift.eu).
  2. Send the questions for Horus (section 8), starting with H1 to H6, and request a test license with a test folder.
  3. Agree the Chift-side conventions in questions C1 to C6 so the connector behaves like the existing ones.
  4. Build the shared components first (connection and token manager, folder rule, reference cache, pagination emulation, VAT catalogue, analytics converter).
  5. Prototype the two riskiest flows against the test folder: invoice retrieval rebuilt from the ledger, and supplier outstanding items computed from matching.
  6. Then implement the remaining routes, using the attribute tables in section 5 as acceptance criteria.

A. Horus endpoint inventory (72 endpoints)Every endpoint in the Horus API reference, what it does, and which Chift route in scope uses it.
EndpointPurposeWritesUsed for (Chift routes in scope)
POST /account-balance.listTrial balance per account for a period (report, period and total balances).NoNone in case scope (would serve Chift's accounts-balance route).
GET /account-histories.infoReturns one ledger line by Id, with VAT, cost and match detail.NoInvoice retrieval, Outstanding items (line-level detail).
POST /account-histories.listGeneral-ledger lines of a folder for a period across all daybooks. Filters: account prefix, daybooks, company, book entry, NotMatchedOnly, ConfirmedOnly, ModifiedAfter. Optional VAT/cost/match/company detail; supports sideloading.NoInvoice retrieval (lines, VAT, analytics, payments via matching); Outstanding items (unmatched lines, due dates, matching); read-back for Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation. VatDetail rebuilds the synthetic tax_code (returned only when exactly one catalogue entry matches; no TaxKind on read). Debit/Credit are assumed to be in the folder currency (assumption).
GET /accounts.infoReturns one account by Id.NoLedger account creation (read-back), Chart of accounts retrieval.
POST /accounts.listLists the GL accounts of a folder (SearchKeys starts-with filter, categories, VAT defaults).NoChart of accounts retrieval (core); Ledger account creation (uniqueness check); Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation (account_number checks).
POST /accounts.newCreates a GL account (SearchKey, Heading, AlternateHeading, personal share); returns Id.YesLedger account creation (core).
POST /accounts.updateUpdates an account's Heading, AlternateHeading or personal share (partial update rule).YesNone in case scope.
POST /book-entries.banks.newCreates a bank operation in a Bank daybook (500): Amount lines of type customer, supplier or general, an optional Tax object on general lines, an optional CODA file.YesNone in case scope.
POST /book-entries.banks.updateFull replacement of a bank operation; returns a NEW id and deletes the attachment.YesNone in case scope.
POST /book-entries.documents.annexes.newAttaches an annex to a book entry that already has a main document (fails otherwise); returns the annexed document's id.YesNone in case scope (would serve Chift's add-attachment route).
POST /book-entries.documents.newAttaches the main document (multipart) to a book entry that has none; takes BookEntryId only.YesInvoice creation (alternative way to attach the PDF after creation).
POST /book-entries.financials.newCreates a financial operation (e.g. a credit-card statement) in a Financial daybook (590).YesNone in case scope.
POST /book-entries.financials.updateFull replacement of a financial operation; returns a NEW id and deletes the attachment.YesNone in case scope.
GET /book-entries.infoReturns one book entry by Id (query Id only; no FolderId, DaybookId or month window). Same object as one book-entries.list element: DocumentNo, Confirmed, Verified, EntryType, OperationType, Date, LastModifiedDate, CompanyDetail (company reference, DueDate, PaymentDate, TotalAmount, TurnoverAmount, TaxAmount, discount) and {Type, Id} references to Folder, Daybook, the main Document and the AccountHistories lines. Lines are references only, and it is not a sideloading endpoint.NoInvoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation (single-call read-back of the returned Id; lines then via account-histories.info or account-histories.list); Outstanding items (optional: resolve an open line's BookEntry reference to its entry header). Dependency: GET attachments (type=invoice|entry: the entry's main Document reference in one call, then documents.download). Folder.Id feeds the cross-folder guard (400 on mismatch). Invoice retrieval does not need it, because book-entries.list returns the same object in bulk. Outside the case scope it would serve Chift's single-invoice route.
POST /book-entries.listEntry headers for one daybook and month window: DocumentNo, Confirmed, EntryType, LastModifiedDate, company detail with totals and due date, main Document, line references. Filters: DocumentNo, ConfirmedOnly, ModifiedAfter. Supports sideloading.NoInvoice retrieval (headers per Sale/Purchase daybook); Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation (duplicate pre-check by Filter.DocumentNo over the fiscal-year period whenever a number is supplied, and before any retry; read-back by Id uses book-entries.info); Outstanding items.
DELETE /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.deleteDeletes an unconfirmed miscellaneous operation that was created through the API.YesNone in case scope. Could roll back a failed Miscellaneous operation creation flow.
POST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.lines.updatePartial update of comment, account, company or MatchNo on miscellaneous operation lines (amounts cannot change); returns the book entry's id.YesNone in case scope.
POST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.newCreates a miscellaneous operation (OD) with balanced Debit/Credit lines (customer, supplier or general), analytics, MatchNo and an optional file. Unconfirmed by default; AutoGenerateNumber only when confirmed.YesMiscellaneous operation creation (core). Confirmed follows the shared posted/draft rule ('posted' → true unless the consumer's 'always push unconfirmed' setting is on).
POST /book-entries.miscellaneous-operations.updateFull replacement of a miscellaneous operation; returns a NEW book entry id and deletes the original attachment.YesNone in case scope.
GET /companies.infoReturns one company by Id.NoClient creation (read-back to build ClientItemOut), Invoice retrieval (partner info), Outstanding items, Invoice creation (partner_id check). Data.Folder.Id feeds the cross-folder guard (400 on mismatch).
POST /companies.listLists the companies (customers, suppliers, beneficiaries) of a folder, with filters including VatNumber, ExternalReference and ModifiedAfter; supports sideloading (Include).NoClient creation (duplicate check on ExternalReference and VAT); Invoice retrieval and Outstanding items (partner data); Invoice creation (partner_info: exact SearchKey match, filtered locally because Filter.SearchKey is a contains filter). Dependency: GET clients/suppliers with search and updated_after.
POST /companies.newCreates a company (IsCustomer/IsSupplier flags, address, IBANs, payment delay, concentration accounts, ExternalReference). Refuses a duplicate VAT number unless AllowDuplicateVatNumber; returns Id.YesClient creation (core); Invoice creation (creates the partner when no company has the partner_info SearchKey). Dependency: POST suppliers.
POST /companies.updatePartial update of a company (null keeps a value, an empty string erases it).YesClient creation: force_merge, i.e. setting IsCustomer=true on an existing supplier; Invoice creation: sets the missing IsCustomer/IsSupplier flag on a reused partner (never '' values). Dependency: POST suppliers force_merge.
POST /cost-histories.listAnalytic ledger lines for a period (debit/credit per cost section, up to 8 dimensions).NoAnalytic accounts retrieval (sums into balance/debit/credit); Invoice retrieval (another source for the analytic distribution).
POST /cost-schemas.infoReturns one cost schema by Id (the Id goes in the body).NoNot needed in case scope: Get analytic plans/accounts read the lists, and the write routes resolve schema search keys only from the folder-scoped cache (no per-call .info). If ever called, check Data.Folder.Id.
POST /cost-schemas.listLists the analytic dimensions (cost schemas) of a folder.NoAnalytic plans retrieval (core); Analytic accounts retrieval (loop over plans); Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation (plan id → schema search key from the per-folder cache). Free schemas (CostSectionType 900) are excluded from Chift analytics in v1.
POST /cost-sections.infoReturns one cost section by Id (the Id goes in the body).NoNot needed in case scope: it takes only an Id and returns no Folder (only CostSchema), so it cannot pass the cross-folder guard. Sections are resolved from the folder-scoped cache.
POST /cost-sections.listLists the cost sections of one schema in a folder.NoAnalytic accounts retrieval (core, one call per List-type schema). Feeds the per-folder cache (Filter.IncludeInactive=true) from which Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation resolve analytic_account ids to section search keys and reject inactive sections (422).
POST /cost-sections.newCreates a cost section in a List-type schema; returns Data.Id.YesNone in case scope (would serve Chift's create analytic account route).
GET /countries.infoReturns one country by Id.NoNone in case scope.
POST /countries.listLists countries (code plus FR/NL/DE/EN names; codes and names filtered with equals).NoClient creation (optional check of the address country code).
POST /customers.outstandingOpen customer ledger lines (receivables) for a folder, optionally per company, with SumDebit/SumCredit.NoOutstanding items (type=client; amount = Debit − Credit, positive = receivable).
GET /daybooks.infoReturns one daybook by Id.NoJournal retrieval, Invoice creation, Miscellaneous operation creation, Outstanding items (journal type). Data.Folder.Id feeds the cross-folder guard on journal_id (400 on mismatch).
POST /daybooks.listLists the journals of a folder: Type enum (which decides the posting endpoint and, through the shared table, the Chift journal_type), IBAN, currency, concentration account.NoJournal retrieval (core); Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation (journal_id checks); Invoice retrieval (loop over Sale/Purchase daybooks); Outstanding items (journal_type).
POST /daybooks.newCreates a daybook (purchase, sale, credit note, bank, financial or misc).YesNone in case scope.
POST /daybooks.updateUpdates a daybook's SearchKey or Heading (partial update rule).YesNone in case scope.
GET /documents.downloadDownloads a document's raw bytes (not JSON).NoDependency: GET attachments (bytes → base64).
GET /documents.infoReturns one document's metadata (type, status, BookEntry).NoInvoice retrieval (attachments_info). Dependency: GET attachments.
POST /documents.listLists the documents of a folder created between two mandatory dates (type, status, linked BookEntry); has no BookEntryId filter.NoInvoice retrieval (attachments_info). Dependency: GET attachments (may help find annexes by BookEntry; to be confirmed).
POST /documents.newUploads a file (Pdf, Xml, CODA, SODA, jpg/jpeg/png) that Horus processes into a book entry in the background; returns a document id.YesNone in case scope (unstructured intake, not a structured invoice create).
GET /fiscal-years.infoReturns one fiscal year by Id, including IsClosed.NoDependency: GET bookyears (the closed flag needs one call per year); closed-year check before posting.
POST /fiscal-years.listLists the fiscal years of a folder (heading, start/end, VAT and per-daybook closure dates; no IsClosed).NoJournal retrieval (next_document_numbers per bookyear); Invoice creation and Miscellaneous operation creation (closure-date check before posting, as Horus advises); Invoice retrieval and Outstanding items (period windows). Dependency: GET bookyears.
GET /folders.infoReturns one folder by Id; adds CountryCode, which the list lacks.NoFolders retrieval (the address country needs one call per folder). Its Language is the write-side language fallback for companies (Client creation, POST suppliers, partner creation in Invoice creation).
POST /folders.listLists accessible folders (Mode 0 all, 1 mine) with contains filters; VatNumber uses equals.NoFolders retrieval (main source). Also validates folder_id for every route, and gives the single-folder fallback when no folder selection is stored (one folder_id rule).
POST /ibans.clearRemoves all IBANs from a company (200 with no body).YesNone in case scope.
POST /ibans.deleteRemoves one IBAN from a company (200 with no body).YesNone in case scope.
GET /ibans.infoReturns one IBAN (Iban, Bic) by Id.NoClient creation (resolves the MainIban and Ibans references into bank_accounts).
POST /ibans.listLists the IBANs of a company (takes CompanyId only, no FolderId).NoClient creation (read-back of bank_accounts). Dependency: GET clients/suppliers bank_accounts.

Unconfirmed by default.YesInvoice creation (supplier_invoice / supplier_refund). Confirmed follows the shared posted/draft rule ('posted' → true unless the consumer's 'always push unconfirmed' setting is on).

POST /invoices.sales.newCreates a sales invoice or credit note (Type 0/1) as a book entry. Confirmed follows the shared posted/draft rule ('posted' → true unless the consumer's 'always push unconfirmed' setting is on).
GET /invoices.sales.outstanding.infoOne open sales invoice, found by FolderId plus BookEntryId or BookEntryNumber ({DaybookSearchKey}-{Number}), with its linked elements.NoInvoice retrieval (payments of one invoice), Outstanding items.

B. Cross-route working assumptionsProposals applied consistently across all route mappings. They are not agreed rules: most depend on an open question in section 8 and will change if the answer differs.
#Working assumptionRationaleDepends on
D1Credit notes: on write, use the credit-note daybook (Horus daybook type 250 for sales, 150 for purchases), falling back to the sales or purchase daybook (type 200 or 100). On read, always scan both daybook types and classify each entry as invoice or credit note from the sign of the customer/supplier line; for open sales items, the documented TransactionType takes precedence.Credit notes posted in a sales daybook (by the connector or by an accountant) must still come back as refunds.H2
D2One synthetic VAT-code id grammar for writing, reading and the VAT-code list: S-{rate}-{origin}-{type} for sales (plus -{country} for OSS lines, origin 13), P-{rate}-{origin}-{type}-{kind}-{condition}-{condition value} for purchases (x for an unused component). On read, return a code only when exactly one catalogue entry matches.Horus has no VAT-code entity, and the purchase “kind” cannot be read back.H1, C2
D3reference and customer_memo share the Horus invoice comment as {reference} | {customer_memo}, split the same way on read.Horus has a single free-text comment on invoices.H9
D4Ledger amounts are treated as folder currency (EUR). EUR amounts are never returned with a foreign currency code.The currency of ledger amounts is not documented.H4
D5Outstanding amounts are signed by side: debit minus credit for customers, credit minus debit for suppliers (positive means owed).One consistent sign per side across all code paths.C5
D6One daybook-type to journal-type table for all routes. Daybook types without a documented posting endpoint are returned with Chift blocked = true.Journals, invoices, operations and outstanding items must agree.H14
D7folder_id is required when a connection has several selected folders.Accounting-firm licenses hold many folders.C12
D8One analytics cache per folder, largest-remainder rounding, and “free” cost schemas excluded in v1.Free schemas have no listable values.H13, C9
D9One shared mapping from a Horus company to a Chift client/supplier/partner, used by client creation, invoice retrieval and invoice creation: active = NOT CustomerActivitySuspended (assumption; meaning undocumented); one “main” address from Address (+Address2), ZipCode, Locality, CountryCode; phone = ContactPhone, email = ContactEmail; is_company always true (Horus does not store it); language = lowercase Horus language, “XX” → null; on write, a missing or unsupported language falls back to the folder language; account_number = concentration account search key.The same company must look the same in every route.H11
D10partner_info on invoice creation reuses the client-creation rules (search key, role flags).Avoid duplicate or half-configured companies.C8
D11The verdict definitions of section 2 are applied identically to every route.Comparable verdicts across routes.n/a
D12“draft” → unconfirmed; “posted” → confirmed, with a per-consumer option to always push unconfirmed. Duplicate check on the document number before every create that supplies an invoice or operation number.Accountant review is the Horus default for API entries.C1, C6, H3
D13Every call by id checks that the object belongs to the requested folder.Calls by id take no folder, so an id from another folder of the same license would otherwise be accepted.H6, H16
Prepared by Silas Deblanc as part of the Chift product/tech case. Based only on the case description, the Chift API specification and the Horus Software API documentation listed in section 2. Proposals in this report are analysis, not commitments on behalf of Chift or Horus.
